Title: Amoghapāśakalparāja
Published: in installments in Annual of The Institute for Comprehensive Studies of Buddhism, Taisho University by Mikkyō Seiten Kenkyūkai
Source: codex unicus from China
Input: Péter Tongori
Amoghapāśakalparāja – Part I
1b(P.1b1, D.1b1)
1. 	namaḥ sarvabuddhabodhisatvebhyaḥ // namaḥ [ā]ryāvalokiteśvarāya bodhisatvāya mahāsatvāya // evam mayā śrutam ekasmin samaye bhagavān potalake parvate viharati sma // avalokiteśvarasya bhavane anekasālatamālacampakāśokātimuktakaḥ / nānāvṛkṣasamalaṅkṛto bhikṣusaṃghena sārddham aṣṭābhir bhikṣusahasrair navanavati-
2. 	bhiś ca bo[dhi]sattvakoṭīniyutaśatasahasrair anekaiś ca śuddhāvāsakāyikair devaputraśatasahasraiḥ parivṛtaḥ puraskṛta īśvaramaheśvarabrahmadevaputrān adhikṛtya dharmaṃ deśayati sma // atha khalv āryāvalokiteśvaro bodhisatvo mahāsattva utthāyāsanād ekāṃsam uttarāsaṅga<ṃ>ṅ kṛtvā dakṣiṇa<ṃ>ñ jānumaṇḍalaṃ pṛthi-
3. 	vyāṃ pratiṣṭhāpya yena bhagavāṃs tenāñjaliṃ praṇamya prahaśitavadano bhūtvā bhagavantam etad avocat / amogharājanāmahṛdayaṃ yan mayā pūrvaṃ ekā(ka)navatime kalpe vilokitāyāṃ lokadhātau lokendrarājñā tathāgatasya sakāśād udgṛhītva(ta)ṃ yena me īśvaramaheśvarān devaputra / pramukhaṃ kṛtvā ba-
4. 	hūni śuddhāvāsakāyikādevaputrasahasrāṇi samādāpitāny anuttarāyāṃ samyaksambodhau asammohajñānavyūhapramukhāni mayā daśasamādhiśatasahasrāṇi pratilabdhāni // yasmiṃś ca punar bhagavān pṛthivīpradeśe idaṃ hṛdayaṃ pracaret / veditavyaṃ bhagavaṃs tatra pṛthivīpradeśe īśvarama-
5. 	heśvarabrahmapramukhāni dvādaśadevaputrasahasrāṇi rakṣāvaraṇaguptaye sthāsyanti caityasammataṃ bhagavān pṛthivīpradeśaṃ bhaviṣyati yatredaṃ / amoghasampadaṃ hṛdayam pracariṣyati // anekabuddhakoṭīniyutaśatasahasrāvaropitakuśalamūlās te bhaga[va]n satvā bhava(vi)ṣya[n]ti / ya iha amoghapā-
6. 	śahṛdayaṃ śroṣyati yaḥ kaścid<a> bhagavan kilbiṣakāriṣyāt pāpadharmasamācāraḥ / āryā[pavā]dakaḥ saddharmapratikṣepakāḥ / avi(vī)ciparāyaṇakaḥ sarvabuddhabodhisattvaśrāvakapratyekabuddhapratikṣiptaḥ sa ca vipratisāraṅ gacchet / āyatyāṃ samvaram āpadyeta tasyaiva tāvad bhagavan ekaikopavāsajāpena ihaiva
7. 	janmani tat karmaṃ viśudhyante kṣaya<ṃ>ṅ gacchaṃti vānti- bhavanti / ekāhikena jvareṇa vā dvaitīyakeṇa vā traitīyakena vā cāturthakena vā ekonasaptāhikena jvareṇa akṣisūlena vā karṇaśūlena vā nāsāśūlena vā danto(ntau)ṣthaśūlena vā jihvātāluśūlena vā hṛdaya- śūlena vā udaraśūlena vā pārśvakaṭiśūle-
2a (P. 2b5, D.2b4)
1.	na vā aṅgapratyaṅgaśūlena vā galagrahaṇī<d>[śūlena] vātī(ti)- sāreṇa vā / hastapādena vā / nāsāśirorū(ru)jāyāṃ vā [ki]lāka(sa)śvitrakuṣṭhavicarccikakiṭibhalohaliṅgabhagandaravisphoṭakāpasmārakākhorda anye vā kṛtyā vadhabandhanatāḍanatarjana abhūtyā(tya)bhyākhyāne vā saṃkṣepataḥ / bhagavan kāya / pīḍitacittapīḍāyāṃ vā duḥsvapnadarśanena vā tat karma pari-
2.	kṣayaṅ gacchati / paryādānaṃ prāg evā(va) śuddhasatvānāṃ śraddhādhimuktikānāṃ yadi bhagavan catasro parṣadaḥ / catvāro varṇā māyāsāthyenāpi ye idam madīyam amoghapāsahṛdayaṃ śroṣyanti / udgrahīṣyanti dhārayiṣyanti vācayiṣyanti likhiṣyanti likhāpayiṣyanti paryavāpsyanti / antaśas tiryagyonigatānāṃ karṇapuṭe sthitvā karṇajā-
3.	po dāśyanti imāni vajrapadāni cintayiṣyanti / aprakṣopataḥ arūpataḥ / avikalpataḥ / asaṃprabhavataḥ / aciraṅgamataḥ / akaraṇataḥ / nirlepataḥ / samacittavigataḥ / virahitapañcaskandhataḥ / anena yogena buddhānusmṛtir bhāvayitavyā ta[d]deśān daśabhyo digbhyaḥ / buddhasahasrapramukhan darśanaṅ ka-
4.	riṣyanti / atyayadeśanāñ ca kariṣyanti peyālam // pustakalikhitām vā kṛtvā gṛhe vā dhārayiṣyanti / svāmibhayena vā parānuvṛtyā vā uccaghnahetunā vā śroṣyanti jñātavyaṃ bhaga[va]n paṇḍitena āryāvalokiteśvarasyānubhāvena teṣāṃ karṇapuṭe tacchabdan nipatiṣyati / tadyathāpi nāma bhagavan ka-
5.	ścid eva puruṣaḥ / candanam vā karpūram vā kā(ka)stūrikām vā  ākrośya paribhāṣya silāyāṃ pe(pi)śya ātmānaṃ lepayet / na tasya candanasya karpūrasya vā kastūrikasya vā na evam bhavati / anenāham ākruṣṭaḥ paribhāṣye vā<d> gandhenāpakramiṣyāmaḥ / api ca sugandha eva saḥ / evam eva bhagavan / idam ma-
6.	dīyaṃ / amoghapāsahṛdayaṃ yaḥ kaścid uccaghya ullapya<ḥ> peyālam // yān māyāśāṭhyenāpi pūjayet teṣām bhagavan khaṭuṅkasatvānāṃ sa eva kuśalahetur bhaviṣyati / yatra yatrotpadyate avirahitaś ca bhaviṣyanti śīlasamādhiprajñāpuṇyasaṃbhāragatena śīlasaugandhikām eva karoti / yaś ca bhaga[va]n kulaputro vā kuladuhi-
7.	tā vā bhikṣur vā bhikṣuṇī vā upāsako vā upāsikā vā tadanyo vā kaścit satvā amoghapāsahṛdayam uddiśya śuklāṣṭamyām upavāsa kuryāt saptavārān amoghapāsahṛdayam anālapataḥ parivartayan tasya bhaga[va]n dṛṣṭa eva dharme ekaviṃśati / anuśansāḥ pratikāṃkṣitavyāḥ katame ekaviṃśati yaduta rogā
2b (P3b4, D.3b2)
1.	cāsyā(sya) ko(kā)ye notpadyante / utpannā cāsya rogāḥ karmavaśena sīghraṃ praśamaṃ yāśyanti / snigdhamanojñagātrāś ca bhaviṣyanti / bahujanapriyaś ca bhaviṣyanti / guptendriyaḥ / atha pratilambhā cāsya bhaviṣyanti / utpannā cāsya(syā)rthā na ca corā vipralāpsyanti nāgninā dahyante nodakena hṛyante / na rājā saknoti sahasā apaharttaṃ / karmāntā cāsya sphī-
2.	tā bhaviṣyanti / nāśini nondura(daka)bhayaṃ bhaviṣyanti / na vātavṛṣṭibhayaṃ saptavārā amoghapāsahṛdaya(ye)na bhasmodakena pariya(ja)pya digvidiśā adhaūrdhvakṣetrasya bandha dātavyam / sarve upadravā upasamiṣyanti / na cojohārā ojaṃ harttaṃ śaknuvanti / sarvasatvānāṃ ca priyo bhaviṣyanti mana āpaś ca / śatrubhayañ cāsya na bhaviṣyati / utpa-
3.	nnā cāsya śatrubhayaṃ śīghraṃ prasamaṃ yāśyanti / na cāsya amanuṣyabhayam bhaviṣyati / na kākhordabhayan na ḍākinībhayan na cāsya tivrā kleśopakleśā bhaviṣyanti / nāgniśastraviśeṇa vā kālaṃ kariṣyati / devatā cāsya satatasamitaṃ rakṣāvaraṇaguptaye sthāsyanti / yatra yatropapatsyante avirahitaś ca
4.	bhaviṣyati / maitrīkaruṇāmuditopekṣāyā ime viṃśatyā(tya)nusamsā tasya pratikāṃkṣitavyāḥ / apare [']ṣṭau dharmā pratilapsyante / katamāny aṣṭau maraṇakāle āryāvalokiteśvaro bhikṣurūpeṇa sammukhan darśanan dāsyati / sukhena kālaṅ kariṣyati / na bhrāntadṛṣṭiś ca bhaviṣyati / na hastavikṣepa
5.	na pādavikṣepan noccāraprasrāvan na mañcārūḍho kālaṅ kariṣyati / sūpasthitismṛtir bhaviṣyati / nādhomukhaṃ kālaṃ kariṣyati / maraṇakāle akṣayapratibhānam bhaviṣyati / yatra cāsya buddhakṣetre praṇidhis tatropapattir bhaviṣyati / avirahitaś ca bhaviṣyati / kalyāṇamitre dine dine trilkā(skā)lan trīn vārān pari<va>-
6.	vartayitavyāḥ / madyamāmsapalāṇḍugṛñjanakālaśunasaṅkaran kṛtañ ce(co)cchiṣṭaṃ viśeṣārthinā hyai(hy e)te varjyāḥ // idaṃ ca amoghapāsahṛdayaṃ dharmaparyāyaṃ sattvānām balābalaṃ jñātvā śrāvayitavyam / ācāryamuṣṭi na kartavyaṃ yasmād vigatamala ātsaryerṣyāpagatā bodhisatvā bhavi[ṣya]nti sattvānām arthakaraṇena / buddhabodhi prāpsyante bo-
7.	dhisatvānāṃ ca gaṇanaṅ gacchati / bodhirū(r u)cyate prajñā satvā iti / upāya etau dvau dharmau satvānām naiva prāpyate sacen me bhagavān anujānīyād yad ahan tad<a> hṛdayan tathāgatasya purataḥ kīrttayeyañ catasṛṇām parṣadām arthāya hitāya sukhāya anyeṣāñ ca pāpakāriṇām // atha khalu bhagavān āryāvalokiteśvaraṃ bodhisa-
3a (P4b3. D.4a7)
1.	tvam etad avocat / bhāṣa tvaṃ śuddhasattva<ya>syedāniṅ kālam manyase / anumoditan tathāgatena paścime kāle paścime samaye bodhisattvayānikānāṃ pitṛkāryaṃ kariṣyati // atha khalv āryāvalokiteśvaro bodhisatvo 'nimiṣanayano bhūtvā bhagavantam etad avocat // śṛṇu me bhagavan sarvabodhisattvanamaskṛtaṃ vimokṣamaṇḍalaṃ
2.	bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya lokānukampāyai mahato janakāyasyārthāya // [1] namas tryadhvānugatapratiṣṭhitebhyaḥ sarvabuddhabodhisattvebhyaḥ / namaḥ sarvapratyekabuddhāryaśrāvakasaṃghebhyo [']tītānāgatapratyutpannebhyaḥ / namaḥ samyaggatānāṃ samyakpratipannānāṃ namah śāradvatīputrāya namo maitreyapramukhebhyo
3.	sarvabodhisattvebhyaḥ mahābodhisattvagaṇebhyaḥ / nam[a] āryāmitābhāya tathāgatāyārhate samyaksambuddhāya namo ratnatrayāya nama āryāvalokiteśvarāya bodhisattvāya mahāsattvāya mahākāruṇikāya // tadyathā oṃ cara cara ciri ciri / curu curu mahākāruṇikaḥ / ciri ciri miri
4.	miri curu curu mahākāruṇikaḥ siri siri ciri ciri / siri siri ciri ciri mahāpadmahastaḥ kala kala kili kili kulu kulu mahāśuddhasattvaḥ / budhya budhya dhāva dhāva kaṇa kaṇa kiṇi kiṇi paramaśuddhasattva kara kara kiri kiri kuru kuru mahāsthāmaprāptaḥ / cala cala saṃcala saṃcala vica-
5.	la vicalaḥ / eṭuṭu eṭuṭu bhara bhara bhiri bhiri bhuru bhuru ehy ehi mahākāruṇikamahāpaśupativesadharaḥ / dhara dhara sara sara cara cara hara haraḥ / hāhā hāhā hihi hihi hūhū hūhū oṃkārabrahmaveśadharaḥ / dhara dhara dhiri dhiri dhuru dhuru bhara bhara sara sara para para cara cara raśmiśatasaha-
6.	srapratimaṇḍitaśarīrāya / jvala jvala tapa tapa bhagavan somādi- tyayamavaruṇakuberabrahmendrariṣidevagaṇābhyarcitacaraṇaḥ suru suru curu curu muru muru puru puru sanatkumārarudravāsavaviṣṇudhanadadevariṣināyakahahuvividhavesadharaḥ / dhara dhara dhiri dhiri dhuru dhuru thara thara ghara ghara yara yara lara lara /
7.	hara hara mara mara para para vara vara varadāyakasamantāvalokitaḥ / vilokitalokeśvaramaheśvaraḥ / muhu muhu muru muru muya muya muñca muñca bhagavān āryāvalokiteśvara rakṣa rakṣa śrītribhuvanadevyāṃ māṃ sarvasatvāṃś ca sarvabhayebhyaḥ sarvopadravebhyaḥ sarvopa[sa]rgebhyaḥ sarvagrahebhyaḥ badhabandhanatāḍanatarjanarā-
3b (P5a7, D.5a5)
1.	jacorataskarāgnir udakaviṣaśastraparimocakaḥ kaṇa kaṇa kiṇi kiṇi kuṇu kuṇu cara cara indriyabalabodhyaṅgacaturāryasatyasaṃprakāśakaḥ / tapa tapa dama dama sama sama / masa masa mahātamo [']ndhakāravidhamanaḥ ṣaṭpāramitāparipūrakaḥ / mili mili / ṭaṭa ṭaṭa / ṭhaṭha ṭhaṭha / ṭiṭi ṭiṭi / ṭuṭu ṭuṭu / eṇeyacarmakṛtaparikaraḥ
2.	/ ehy ehi īśvaramaheśvarabhūtagaṇabhañjakaḥ kuru kuru para para kara kara / kaṭa kaṭa maṭa maṭa viśuddhaviṣayanivāsinaḥ / mahākāruṇika śvetayajñopavitaratnamakuṭamālādharaḥ sarvajñaśirasikṛtamakuṭamālādharaḥ mahādbhūtakamalakṛtakarataladhyānasamādhivimokṣāprakampyaḥ / bahusattvasantati-
3.	paripācakaḥ mahākāruṇikaḥ sarvakarmāvaraṇaviśodhakaḥ sarvā(rva)vyādhipramocakaḥ sarvāśāparipūrakaḥ sarvasattvasamāsvāsakaḥ namo [']stu te svāhā // paṭhitasiddhasya karmāṇi bhavanti triḥkālajāpena pañcānantaryāṇi viśodhayati / sarvakarmāvaraṇapariśuddhiñ ca karoti / agarudhū-
4.	pena sīmābandho bhasmodakena sarṣapakhadirakīlakādyaiḥ sarvajvareṣu sūtrakaṃ sarvavyādhiṣu ghṛtan tailam udakam vā pariya(ja)pya dātavyaṃ / kākhordacchedanī śastreṇa rakṣāśūtrakena udaraśūle lavaṇodakaṃ viṣanāśanaṃ mṛ<r>ttikāyā udakena vā cakṣuroge śvetasūtrakaṃ karṇe bandhitavyaṃ danta-
5.	śūle karavīradantakāṣṭhaṃ sīmābandhaṃ pañcaraṅgikasūtram ekaviṃśativārān pariya(ja)pya caturṣu khadirakīlakeṣu badhvā caturddiśan nikhantavyā mahāsīmābandhā bhavanti / sarvarakṣā śūtrakenodakena bhasmanā vā sarvagraheṣu pañcaraṅgikasūtrakaṃ / sarvajvareṣu śvetasūtrakam / sarvakīṭalūtalohali-
6.	ṅgagalagraheṣu madhupippalīyutaṃ cakṣuroge gandhodakaṃ palāśodakaṃ madhuyasto(ṣtyu)dakam vā karṇaśūle tailaṃ sarvakalikalahavigrahavivādā[bhyā]khyāneṣūdakaṃ pariya(ja)pya mukhaṃ prakṣālayitavyaṃ / balaviṣayarājyarāṣṭraṃ rakṣeṣu pūrṇakā sthāpayitavyā śucinā śucivastraprāvṛtena mahatīpūjāṃ kṛtvā vācayitavyaṃ mahāsā-
7.	ntir bhavati tena codakena siñcitavyā sarvasattvānāṃ rakṣā kṛtā bhavati / sarva itvo(tv u)padravopasarggāḥ śāntim udṛtānāṃ candanavilepanaṃ hṛdayena ekaviṃśativārān pariya(ja)pya kartavyaṃ sarvānantaryakṣapaṇī satatajāpena gṛharakṣā padmahomena sarvasattvānāṃ vartanaṃ candanahomena sarvabhūtagraha-
4a (P.6a6, D.6a3)
1.	rakṣā / jayā vijayā nākuli gandhānākuli vāri(ru)ṇī abhayapāṇi indrapāṇi gandhapriyaṅgu tagara cakrā mahācakrā viṣṇukrāntā somarājī sunandā aṣṭot[t]aravāraṃ śataṃ pariya(ja)pya maṇiṃ kṛtvā śirasi bāhau bandhā dhārayitavyāḥ / bālānāṃ gale nārīṇāṃ vilagna snapanaṃ paraṃ / saubhāgyakaraṃ alakṣmīpraśa[ma]nam etena maṇinā bandhitaḥ sarvarakṣā kṛ-
2.	tā bhavati / viṣāgnir na kramati viṣakṛto notpadyate / utpannā na piḍāṃ janayiṣyati / śīghraṃ prasamiṣyanti / grahā prasamiṣyanti va(vā)tameghāśanistambhanam udakena vāraṇīlatayā sarvakaram / āryāvalokiteśvarahṛdayaṃ paramasiddhim āsādhita etāni karmāni kurute // atha sādhayitum icchad vidhiḥ paṭake aśleṣakair aṅgai[r] buddhapratimā likhya āryā-
3.	valokiteśvaraḥ / jaṭāmukuṭa eṇeyacarmapaśupativeṣadharaḥ sarvālaṅkāravibhūṣitaḥ poṣadhikena citrakareṇa citr[a] āpayitavyas tataḥ sādhako(ke)na tasyāgrato pratimāyā gomayamaṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā śvetapuṣpāvakīrṇa aṣṭau pūrṇakāḥ sthāpayitavyā aṣṭau <r>upahārā catuḥṣaṣṭir upahārānye
4.	balimānsarudhiravivarjitā agarudhūpayatā vidyā aṣṭasahasra japayitavyāḥ / ahorātroṣitena trirātroṣitena triśuklābhojana triḥkālasnāyinā śucivastraṃ prāvṛte svayam evāryāvalokiteśvara āgacchati / sarvāsāṃ paripūrayati / manaḥśilā añjanam vā parijapya akṣiṇy a-
5.	ñjayitvā tato [']ntarhito bhavati / ākāśena prakramati / amoghajñānavyūhaṃ nāma samādhiṃ pratilapsyate yan icchati tat karoty ata[s] so(sā)dhanam / idam avocad bhagavān āttamanā āryāvalokiteśvaro bodhisattvas te ca śuddhāvāsakāyikā devaputrā brahmasahā<ṃ>patiīśvaramaheśvara bhagavan bhāṣi-
6.	tam abhya(bhyā)nandan // // amoghapāsaṃ samāptam // atha khalv āryāvalokiteśvaro bodhisattvo mahāsattva utthāyāsanād ekāṃsam uttarāsaṅgaṃ kṛtvā dakṣiṇañ jānumaṇḍalaṃ pṛthivyāṃ pratiṣṭhāpya sahasrajvālakiraṇasūryo[j]jvalanānāraśmivinirgataśarīraṃ yena bhagavāṃs tenāñjalim praṇamya vika-
7.	sitasaumyapa- // // ripūrṇamaṇḍalamukhaprahasi- // // tavadano bhūtvā mahāmaitrīkaruṇābalam <vya>manasikārā cāturvarṇasya lokasya hitakaruṇāsarvasiddhim anuttaraṃ vidyādharaṇāṃ tattvasiddhayavarapradāpratilambhārthaṃ bhaga[va]ntam etad avocat / asti mama bhagavann idam amogharājaṃ mahā-
4b (P.7a5, D.7a2)
1.	kalparājahṛdayaṃ tatredam asya padmodāramaṇḍalasya mahātattvasārodgatasārabhūtaṃ tattvasiddhiparamamahāmaṇḍalamudrāmantrahṛdayapaṭalaguhyaṃ paramavimokṣamaṇḍalaṃ samayasādhanarahasyaṃ tad bhagavataḥ purataḥ parikīrtayiṣye / iha ca mahati mahāparṣanmadhye devanāgayakṣagandharvāsuragaruḍakinnaramahoragaḥ purataḥ siddhavidyādevata(tā)maṇḍa-
2.	lāniṣṭāpurataḥ parikīrtayāmi yena īśva[ra]maheśvarabrahmadevatāyamavaruṇakuberaṛ<i>ṣigaṇacandrasūryatārāgaṇanakṣatrasiddhavidyādharadevīnāṃ purataḥ / tattvasārabhūtahṛdayalokottarasādhanasiddhiḥ paṭhitasādhanabhāvanasiddhi bhagavataḥ purataḥ / mahāvidyādharagaṇaparivāraiḥ purataḥ parikīrtayāmi / tattvasāraguhyapara-
3.	maṃ bhāṣiṣye // atha khalu bhagavān śākyamunis tathāgata śatasahasrapuṇyālaṃkṛtalakṣaṇānuvyañjanavirājitaiḥ suvarṇavarṇabāhur anekai raśmijvālāmālagandhanāgasadṛśaśuṇḍa prasārya kāre mūrdhasire āryāvalokiteśvarasya bodhisattvasya mahāsattvasya sthāpya parimārjaitad avocat / bhāṣasva mahā-

3.	ghamaṇi svāhā // bhūṣaṇālaṅkāramantra saptajaptayā // [69] oṃ oṃ padmakare vipulanakhāṅguli samasamasarvatathāgatādhiṣṭhite svāhā // añjalīmantra ekaviṃśatijaptayā // [70] oṃ sarvaguṇo(na)pratihatasirovandanayā namaskaromi mili mili turiṇi svāhā // namaskāramantra saptajaptayā // [71] oṃ daśadiśābhivandanaprasa-
4.	ratu hūṃ // pradakṣiṇamantra ekaviṃśatijaptayā // [72] oṃ vividhāyudhajvalitapāṇi prajvala sphura hūṃ // praharaṇamantra ekaviṃśatijaptayā // [73] oṃ divyāmoghavipulasvare madhuranighoṣarute raṇa raṇa hūṃ // ghaṇṭāmantra ekaviṃśatiya(ja)ptayā / / atha vāṣṭottaraśataṃ ceti // [74] oṃ raśmijvālasamantenāvabhāsaya mahāmaṇiratnaśikhe svāhā // dīpamantra aṣṭo-
5.	ttaraśatajaptayā // [75] oṃ dhuru dhuru āvāhaya śīghrajave hūṃ // āvāhanamantraḥ // gugguladhūpena aṣṭaśatajaptena // [76] oṃ duṣṭasattvavibodhani samasamayadhare svāhā // samayamantra ekaviṃśatijaptayā // [77] oṃ vicitramaṇi vipulapravartani hūṃ // akṣamālāmantra aṣṭottaraśatajaptayā // [78] oṃ vicitraratno[j]jvaladivyakusumadhare puṣpara-
6.	ti svāhā // māla(lā)mantra śaptajaptayā // [79] oṃ divyalocane amoghe pravare sarvavidyādhiṣṭhitasiddhe śuddhavilokite svāhā // āvāhanamantra ekaviṃśatijaptayā // [80] oṃ amoghapadme samantabhuvane tara tara gacchasva bhavanaṃ svāhā // visarjanamantra eka(ka)viṃśatijaptayā // [81] oṃ divyasaṃpūraṇi nāgavilokani hūṃ // khakhorakamantra ekaviṃśatijapta-
7.	yā // [82] oṃ pātrapūraṇi pūraya amoghavati sama[nta]prabhe svāhā // pātramantra ekaviṃśatijaptayā // [83] oṃ durbha[ra]saṃ[s]tara vidyādhiṣṭhita prasara sura sura ṛṣipūjite svāhā // durbharasaṃstaraṇamantra ekaviṃśatijaptayā // [84] oṃ jvaladhūmajvālaśikhiraśmigarbhe svāhā // homamantra aṣṭottaraśatajaptayā // [85] oṃ padmini padmāsane varapravare nāgabhuvane svāhā //
11a (P17a4, D.17b3)
1.	nadīsaramantra saptajaptayā // [86] oṃ khacāriṇi gama gamaya pādapracāriṇi hūṃ // upādānamantra aṣṭottaraśatajaptayā // [37] oṃ sarvatrāmoghābhiṣekamahācūḍāvimale abhiṣiñcantu māṃ pravarapāṇibhuje padmadhvaje svāhā // abhiṣekamantra ekaviṃśatijaptayā // etāvan matrāṃ(mantrān) pṛthakpṛthak sarvāṃ yathākalpavidhisādhanaṃ vidyādhareṇa pṛthak<a> sarvāṃ / anu-
2.	yojya sādhanāvidhi etān pṛthak sarvakāryeṣu mahāvipule prabhāvavibhūtayo puṣpadhūpagandhamālyavilepanair <n>nānāratnabhājanavicitrair <m>maṇivimānavipulaiḥ prādurbhavanti / nānādivyapuṣpapaṭṭapatākāḥ prādurbhavanti / nānārasarasāgra- // // tāṃ śatarasabhojanavarāṃ prādurbhavanti // nānādivyabalividhānāni prādurbhavanti / yathā-
3.	rhāṇi tathāgatavimānāni bhavanti / yathā bodhisattvavimānāni prādurbhavanti / yathā devānāṃ vimānāni tathā prādurbhavanti / yathā nāgānāṃ yakṣāṇāṃ asurāṇāṃ garuḍakinnaramahoragāṇāṃ vimānāni tathā svakasvakāni prādurbhavanti / yathā yathā mānuṣyakānāṃ vimānāni bhojanavaraṃ prādurbhavati / evaṃ vidhayaḥ
4.	sarvakāryāṇi karaṇiyāni tattvasādhanayathoktāni kalpavidhimantrasādhanāni kartavyāni / tato vidyādhareṇa sarvasiddhim anuttaram anuprāpnoti / yadi vidyādhara saṃpūrṇaṃ sarvakāryāṇi kariṣyati tad aham udiritimātrayā amoghapāsahṛdaya tasya vidyādharasya svaru(rū)peṇa agratas upatiṣṭhāmi sarvāsāma-
5.	norathaṃ paripūrayiṣyāmi sarvapāpāvaraṇakilbiṣa nāśayāmi mahādivyakāyapariśuddhiṃ śodhayiṣyāmi sarvakalpamudra(drā)manuhṛdayamaṇḍala / amogharājavimokṣamaṇḍalaṃ paramasiddhim anupradāsyāmi / mahāvidyādharacakravarti abhiṣekābhiṣiñcayāmi / tattvabhūtasādhanasiddhim anupradāsyāmi / mahāmoghapā-
6.	śalaukikalokottarasādhanasiddhim anupradāsyāmi / suvipulavaiśāradyāni mahāpūjāsatkārasarvaloka uttamavisiṣṭaṃ / arthāgamadivyaṃ śriyam anupradāsyāmi / nātra kāṃkṣā na vimatir ū(u)tpādayitavyaṃ / na cānyathā bhāvamanasikārāṃ pravartayitavyaṃ / maitrīkaruṇāmuditopekṣāṃ sarvasattvadayāpareṇa bhavitavyam / ekaputrakam iva sarvasa-
7.	tvabhūteṣu dayāpareṇa bhavitavyam / satyavādī satyarana(ta)ḥ priyavādī karuṇādhyāsayenāvalambya amoghapāsahṛdayaṃ smārayitavyam / jāpavidhī kartavyam / mudrā mantrañ ca pravartayitavyaṃ / sarvatathāgatān daśasu dikṣu buddhakṣetrāntargatā manasikārā buddhānusmṛtibhāvayitavyam / namaskartavyaṃ / sarvabodhisatvānāṃ namaskartavyaṃ / āryāvalo-
11b (P18a1, D.18b3)
1.	kiteśvaranāthaṃ mukhaṃ dhārayitavyaṃ namaskāraṃ kartavyaṃ / bhagavataḥ āryāvalokiteśvaraṃ paṭaduṣyābhimukhaṃ paryaṅkanisaṇṇena darbhasaṃsta[ra]stṛtena ba[d]dhvā pravartayitavyaṃ / anālāpataḥ // eṣa guhyasādhanatattvataḥ // athāta mudrāmantraṃ pravakṣyāmi amoghapāsahṛdayasya tattvasiddhimāṃ adhiṣṭhitaṃ lokeśvaranāthaṃ kāruṇikaṃ sattvavatsalaṃ suddhasattvaṃ padmahastaṃ sa-
2.	rvasattvakṛpātmakam // adhiṣṭhānaṃ daśadiśe sarvatathāgatānāṃ devā nāgā ca yakṣā ca rākṣasāsuraguhyakā / lokapālā ca brahmā ca rudraviṣṇukumārādiṣu yamaś ca varuṇaś caiva kuberā riṣigaṇas tathā / candrasūryā sanakṣatraṃ tathā īśvaramaheśvaraṃ / ete śīghram āyānti mudrāgrahaṇamātrayā / saptatārā samāyānti tathā pāramitān daśa nīlakaṇṭhī tathā pāṇḍaravāsi-
3.	nī / tathā ca padmanartteśvarī jayāś ca vijayaś caiva śaṃkalī vajraśaṃkalī māmakī bhṛkuṭīdevī anyopamyā ca dyuṭi ca bhīmā śriya(yā) sarasvatyā padmakosthī ca utpalī puṣpadantī tathā kailāsaśīkharavāsini / hārītibhūtamātā ca tathā daśa vajrapāṇimahābalā / yathā vidyādharendrair mahāvajradharas tathā / tṛdaśendraṃ samā devaiś ca dvātṛṃśa-
4.	dbhavadevatā / ete sarve samāyānti mudrāmantre grahaṇamātrayā daśadiśā sarvatathāgatā vilokenti darśanan dāsyanti tatkṣaṇaṃ bodhisattvā vilokenti varadā nityaṃ bhaviṣyati nityaṃ darśanakāmakā // āryāvalokiteśvaran nāthaṃ sahasrabāhūṃ ca sahasranetraṃ nānāmudrāṇidhārakam / mudrāgrahaṇamātreṇa darśanan dāsyati na śaṃsaya-
5.	ḥ / brahmaveśaṃ / amoghapāsaṃ ca nīlakaṇṭhīhayagrīvañ ca tathā vaḍabāmukhaṃ // ekādaśamukhaṃ nāthan tathā caturmukham eva ca // aṣṭabhayaharanāthācintyāvalokitan tathā / nānāvidhāni rūpāṇi māyopamasamādhinā / sarvan darśayati nāthamudrayā grahaṇamātrayā / sukhāvatīlokadhātu amitābhaṃ nāyakaṃ jinam / kṣaṇād darśayate
6.	nātha bodhisattvagaṇaparivṛtam // anyāni buddhakṣetrasahasrāṇi gaṅgānadivālukopamāṃ / koṭīniyutaśatasahasrāṇi sabodhisattvagaṇaparivṛtān tathāgatān darśayate nāthamudrāpravartanamātrayā / anyā vividhamahādarśatāni paśyati dine dine bo[dhi]maṇḍalaṃ dharmacakrañ ca tathā vajrāsanam eva ca māradharma(rṣa)ṇam ity etat paśyate svapnadarśana-
7.	n nātra kāṃkṣā nikurvīta na vimativicikitsā naiva ca // avigarhita buddhair vā pañcānantaryam eva vā / duṣṭacittamahānāthabodhisattva(tvā)ntarāyakā / āryāpavādakāś caiva saddharmasya ca vivācanā / pratikṣiptajinabhāṣyañ ca avicigataparāyaṇam / ṣoḍaśai narakadāruṇe saṃsthitāḥ kalpakoṭayaḥ kṣaṇāḥ sarve parimucyanti // mudrāmantraprava-
12a (P18b7, D.19b3)
1.	rtanamātrayā śravaṇā darśanāś caiva nāmodgrahaṇam eva ca / / sarvam etāni pāpāni naśyante nātra saṃśayaḥ / amoghapāsahṛdayamudrā ca darśanā lokanāthasya tulyam etad viśiṣyate / buddhakoṭīsahasrāṇi gaṅgāvārikaśatasahasrāṇi darśanam bhavet / sarve bodhimaṇḍalagatan dharmacakrapravartanaṃ tulyaṃ / amogharājahṛdayaṃ mudrāyā mantradarśanam etā
2.	mahāpuṇyaskandham avāpnoti puṇyarāśivivardha / vardhate āyuṣā tasya aparimito atra na saṃśayam iti / atha vidyādhareṇa śucir bhūtvā śucivastrāṇi dhārakaḥ pūrvopacāravidhinā śuciḥ śaucācāraṃ sadā kuru nānāgandhaliptāṅgaṃ nānādhūpapradhūpitaḥ / divyā(vya)gandhānulepanaṃ bāhū(hu)pāṇim eva ca / paryaṅkena niṣīditvā / śuci ā-
3.	sanasaṃstṛtā śucibhūmiṣu bhāgañ ca maitracittaṃ samāsthāya karuṇādhyāśayatattvataḥ / namaskṛtvā daśadiśaṃ buddhastryadhvānugatā sadā lokeśvaraguruṃ pūjya dharmacittaparaḥ sadā / anālāpaṃ vidhijñañ ca kṛtarakṣāvidhānataḥ suvidhijñaḥ supraśastañ ca gurugauravatattvataḥ satyavādī priyālāpa sarvasattvakaruṇā-
4.	tmakaḥ sadānukūlañ ca tataḥ / etā mudrā pravartayaṃ padmākāra kare sthāpya sasamāṅguṣṭhāni bandhayam / tarjanyā candrākāra hṛdisthānañ ca sthāpayam / mudreyaṃ amogharājahṛdayaṃ mūlamudrāyā buddhabodhipradāyikāḥ / ākarṣaṇaṃ lokeśvaranātham mantram etām udāharet // [88] oṃ amoghavikurvita samantāvalokaya
5.	māṃ stryadhvādhiṣṭhitā amoghapadme cara vicara sañcara hūṃ hūṃ // samannantare stryadhvā[va]lokitāmoghasphuṭapadmajvalitaprabhāsavikū(ku)rvaṇaṃ mudrārājā stryadhvānugatiḥ samantair buddhakṣetraparamāṇurajaḥ samair mahati mahākampo prādurbhaviṣyati / tena tryadhvānugataḥ pratiṣṭhitā buddhā bhagavanta nānāraśmayo ra(’)nekaśatasahasra-
6.	koṭikāyā niścaranti / sarvais tais tathāgatāḥ saṃpraharṣitamahākaruṇāntargatā buddhamanasikāreṇa suvarṇavarṇaṃ bāhūṃ prasārayati vidyādharasya mūrdhānaṃ sthāpayanti / uttamasiddhiṃ amoghavikurvaṇādhiṣṭhānam adhiṣṭhitāni anupradāsyanti / īśvaramaheśvarabrahmā devaputrā yamavaruṇakuberavāsavasanatkumārādaya devatā ra-
7.	kṣācaraṇaguptaye saṃvidhāsyanti / saha pravartitamātreṇeyaṃ mudrā sarvapāpāvaraṇāni / aṣṭabhir mahādāruṇabhayaiḥ pari<r>mukto bhavati / sarvāvaraṇāni cāsya manoramasiddhir bhavir bhaviṣyati / sarvakarmakaro mudrā vikhyātāḥ sarvatra uttamā iti // // vikasitaṃ padmamudrā samādhāya samayāgrāmoghaviśrutaḥ /
12b (P19b4, D.20b2)
1.	madhyamāṅguṣṭhāpadmāṃkuśākāranakhaṃ sama(mā)sthitam // mudreyaṃ samatākhyātā vimokṣamaṇḍaladāyikā / mudrayā gṛhyamānasya mantram etad udāharana // [89] oṃ amoghapadmāṃkuśasamayaṃ gṛhna gṛhna dhara dhara mahāsattva hūṃ // samanantarapravartitamudrāyā pravartitamātrāyā samana[nta]ragṛhitasya tryadhvānugatā buddhā bhagavantaḥ sādhukāram anupradāśyanti
2.	// sarve te<s> tathāgatās tuṣṭa udagrāḥ pramuditaḥ prītisaumanasya jātā bhaviṣyanti / mahākaruṇāsphuritahṛdayā vidyādharasya uttamasiddhim anupradāsyanti punaḥ puna darśanam anupradāśyanti / sarvavaram anupradāśyanti / amoghasamayasiddhim anupradāsyanti / sarvatathāgataguhyamaṇḍalakalpamudrāmantradhāriṇī vimokṣamaṇḍalagṛ-
3.	hitamātra samayam anupraviṣṭo bhavati / sarvā(rva)trāryāvalokiteśvarasya guhyamaṇḍalasamayajño bhavati / sarvatra kāryamantrasiddhir bhaviṣyati / āryāvalokiteśvaravarado bhavati / nityañ ca darśanakāmado bhavati // padme karatalaṃ kṛtvā madhyamāṅguṣṭhavajrasaṃyutām / kanyasāprasāryan tu mudrā vai padmadharasya ca /
4.	dhūpapuṣpādigandhābhyā mālyavilepanacūrṇāni paṭṭavastrābharaṇam eva ca // balim arghapradānañ ca pūrṇakumbhasāgarādibhiḥ / pañcaraṅgikasūtrañ ca raṅgacūrṇāni eva ca // anyāni vividharūpahārāṇi sarvatreyaṃ mudrām uttamam / nivedanam idaṃ mudrā vāhva(kva)tā sarvatram uttamā / amoghanivedana<ṃ>m idaṃ mudrā padmapāṇir adhiṣṭhi-
5.	tā iti // // eṣaiva mudrayā maṇimadhyā tu veṣṭayaṃ padmaratnaviśāradaḥ / mudreyam uttamasiddhi āvāhanam uttamam // [90] oṃ amoghapadmamahāsattva āyātum iha maṇḍale ciri ciri ciri padmabhuje svāhā adhyeṣaṇās tryadhvānugatā buddhā nimantraṇam eva ca / adhyeṣaṇā sarvabodhisattvānāṃ padmahastani-
6.	mantraṇaṃ // āvāhanaṃ sarvadevānāṃ tathā maṇḍaladevatām // eṣā mudrā pravartanā tu sarve daśadiśā samāyānti buddhā bodhisatvā tathā devā nāgā ca yakṣā ca gandharvā asurās tathā / garuḍā kinnarāś caiva mahoragāḥ sarve te kṣaṇaṃ samāyānti rakṣārthena na saṃśayam iti // vajrāka(kā)raṃ hastapāṇim āsthā-
7.	ya kamyasā padmasandhiṣu madhyamānikuṃcyamātrā tu mudrā bandhaya siddhimāṃ / eṣā diśābandhaṃ maṇḍalabandhañ ca rakṣākarmaviśāradaḥ / eṣā gṛhītamātrā tu mudrayā mantrāni vai japya saha[sra]pravartitamātrā tu samantāḥ saptayojanam / śīmāhandhamaṇḍalabandhaś ca rakṣākarmakṛto bhaviṣya- // tīti // [91] oṃ amoghavajradṛḍhapa-
13a (P.20b1, D.21a7)
1.	dmabandha samantena mahāsīmā rakṣantu māṃ curu curu hūṃ phaṭ // athaḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi amoghamudrā tu siddhimāṃ sarvasattvahitāmoghamudrā tu viśeṣataḥ / vajrāñjalipuṭaṃ badhvā samamadhyonkitāṅguliṃ kaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭhavikacā tarjanyā tu nikuñcitaṃ mahāmoghā viśvarājapadmamudrā tu kīrtitā sarvakarmikamudreyaṃ sarvakarmasuyojayaṃ praviśā mudram ākhyātā sa-
2.	rvamaṇḍalam uttamam // asya gṛhītamātrasya mudrā mantram uttamā rakṣaṇā vidyādharā praviṣṭā vai stryadhvānugatavimokṣamaṇḍalaṃ paramasiddhir bhaviṣyatīti // [92] oṃ vajre vajrādhiṣṭhitapadme viśvarūpadhare dhara dhara hūṃ praviśatu stri(strya)dhvagatāṃ dhiri tuṭi svāhā // eṣā gṛhītāyā mudrāyā tattvasiddhaya kamyate daśadiśā buddhakṣetrā na śaṃsayaḥ / jvalate padma-
3.	pāṇiśarīrasya mahātuṣṭir bhaviṣyati / nityaṃ vai prahasitavadana vidyādharasya darśanā agratam upatiṣṭhati / apādhṛtasvargadvārāṇi buddhakṣetrā samantataḥ / dvātriṃśa[d]bhavanā devās tiṣṭhanti dvārā apāvṛtā trayatriṃśa[d]bhavanair dvāraiḥ sarve tiṣṭhanti apāvṛtaiḥ sukhāvatilokadhātudvārā tiṣṭhanti apāvṛtā sarvabhavanā
4.	praveśedaṃ mudrāgrahaṇamātrayā / sarvapāpaharaṃ mudraṃ (drām) / sarvakleśapradālanaṃ / sarvaduḥkhavinirmuktam sarvakilbiṣanāśanaṃ / nirmuktā ṣoḍaśabhin narakair aṣṭabhir bhayadāruṇaiḥ / mudrāyā pravartanamātrāyā sarve naśyaduṣkṛtā / pithitāni narakā dvārāṇi uttīrṇā te bhayadāruṇaiś ceti / asya darśanamātrā vai mudrayā vidyādharasya
5.	/ sarvapāpā vimucyete pañcānantaryakṛtāni ca / sarvasattvapriyan nityam adhiṣṭhitā vai<ḥ> sarvatathāgatā iti // // eṣaiva mudrā aṅguṣṭhakuñcitāgryāgrahā madhyapadmajaḍe(ṭā)kāra mūrdhijaṭa sthāpya padmajaṭamudreyaṃ / amoghahasta iti viśrutā / mantre tām udīryan tu sarvasattvahitāya vai<ḥ> // [93] oṃ amoghapadmajale
6.	viśodhaya hūṃ viri viri vimale svāhā // mūrdhaṃ sthāpya mudrā vai<ḥ> snāpayed dhāriṇā śubhā / mantraṃ japte aṣṭottaraśataṃ sa- // // dā saha snāpitamātreṇa mudrāmantravisāradaṃ / aśuddhi śuddhim āyānti aśuddhi śuddhir evam ca <ca> // abrahmacī(cā)ri brahmacaryā vai<ḥ> / amauni maunim eva ca // puṇyarāśi jvalate divyaṃ sūryacandravirājite / sa-
7.	rvalokapriyo nityaṃ mantrasiddhir anuttaram / amoghapāsasiddhiṃ vai amoghasiddhitattvataḥ / devatāsarvam āyānti rakṣaṇārthe na saṃśayaḥ // āryāvalokitanāthasya śuddhasattvavaradāyakaḥ sarvatra varadā nityaṃ darśanañ ca dine dine / apurārogya vacanan nityaṃ tuṣṭapuṣṭis tathāpi vā / sarvatra siddhyate karmaṃ sarvavyādhi vimu-
13b (P21a6, D.22a6)
1.	cyate // sarvāvaraṇavinirmuktaḥ sarvamaṅgalaṃ uttamaṃ / snānam etad vicitraṃ vai amoghasnānam uttama iti // // dvayapāṇi dṛḍhīkṛtya maṇipadmam avargatī / tarjanīdvayasaṃkocā samantāyāpi samāhitāḥ / samādhim iva mudreyaṃ paryaṅkaṃ bandhyā niṣīdayam / mudra(drā)m etā pravartata(te) mantrajaptā samāhitaḥ / sagṛhītamātreṇa paryaṅkavajramerusamam bhave-
2.	t / samantakavacitobhon nira(rā)dṛśya sarvavighnavināyakaiḥ / yakṣarākṣasabhūtaiś ca asurair garu- // // ḍam eva ca // sarve te dūratā pralayaṃ yānti diśeṣu vidiśeṣu ca / avighna sidhyate karmaṃ jāpakarma na saṃśayam iti // [94] oṃ amoghavajradṛḍhapadmeśvara hūṃ samāñjalī samādhāya tarjanīmadhyapīḍitā / kavisitā padmāṅguṣṭhamukhayo
3.	mudrā amogha(ghe)śvararājasya mantramudrā pravarteta āryāvalokiteśvarasyāgrataḥ / [95] oṃ padmeśvara amoghavarada bhiri bhiri svāhā // anena prathamato jāpye(pyai)kaviṃśati / tato mudrān tu bandhayaṃ amoghapāśahṛdayaṃ japya ekāgrā tattvam āśayaḥ / ekavelā samāsmāritamātreṇa kampyate stryadhvānugataṃ sadā bu-
4.	ddhakoṭiśatasahasragaṅgānadīvālukā samā tathāgatā arhantaḥ samyaksaṃbuddha tasya vidyādharasya sādhukāram anupradāśyanti / āśvāsayanti / nānāraśmibhi taṃ vidyādharam anukṣepsyanti / āryāvalokiteśvaranātha saha smāritamātrayā agratam upatiṣṭhati / atha gṛhītamātreṇa ṣaṭ pāramitā paripūrṇī-
5.	bhavati / daśapāramitāsamanvāgato bhavati / aṣṭādaśaveṇika(kā)buddhadharmasamanvāgato bhavati / daśatathāgatabalasamanvāgato bhavati / catvāri vaiśāradyāni pratilabhate / āryāṣṭāṅgamārgaparisuddhiṃ pratilabhate / eṣa mudrāgrahaṇamātreṇa amogharājahṛdayasmaraṇamātreṇa mahati guṇānuśamsān pratilabhate
6.	aṣṭottaraśatajaptena aṣṭottaraśatamudrāpravartanena / ekenopa- vāsajāpena āryāvalokiteśvaraṃ svābhāvikenātmabhāvena agratam upatiṣṭhati vidyādharasya sādhukāran dāsyati / sādhu sādhu vidyādhara eṣa paramajāpa dakṣiṇabāhuṃ prasārya pāṇiṃ vidyādharasya mūrdha sthāpayati samāśvāsayati / ehi putraka vyūhi(ha)varaṃ yadartha-
7.	n dadāmi(mī)ti / anugṛhyā<a>dhiṣṭhita tvaṃ vidyādharajyeṣṭhaputro mama bhavasveti / siddha tvayā vidyādhara amoghapāsahṛdayaṃ vimokṣamaṇḍalamudrāpaṭalakalparājadhīreṇa bhavasveti / mahākaruṇācittena sarvasattvānāṃ bhavasveti / eṣa tvayā paścimakagarbhāvāsaś cyutattvaṃ sukhāvatīlokadhātum anuvrajasva upapādukapadmebhyo prajāśyate
14a (P22a5, D.23a5)
1.	sarvāvaraṇavibhūśit lakṣaṇānuvyañjanapratimaṇḍitaḥ / aparimitāyuṣaś ca bhaviṣyati / yāvad bodhimaṇḍalaparyavasānād iti // ata aṣṭottarasahasrajāpena aṣṭottaraśatamudrāpravartanena anālayataḥ pūrṇe aṣṭottarasahasreṇākāśenāgacchati / amoghavikurvaṇaviśuddhimaṇḍalacakravartir bhaviṣyati / aṣṭādaśavidyādharakoṭi-
2.	niyutaśatasahasraparivāro bhaviṣyati / aparamitāyuś ca bhaviṣyati / mahāmoghavipulaprabhāsan nāma ṛddhiṃ pravartate / yena riddhyānubhāvena trisāhasramahāsāhasro lokadhātubāhuṃ sammiñjitaprasāritamātrayāgacchati / śatasahasrajāpe śatasahasramudrā pravartate [’]nena ihaiva janmani āryāvalokiteśva-
3.	rasadṛśo bhavati / māyopamasamādhinā vikurvate divyaṃ amoghacakṣu pariśuddhaṃ pratila[bha]te / īśvaramaheśvarabrahmā devaputrarudravāsavasomādityakumārayamavaruṇakuberādaya vividharūpa darśayati yāvad vividhanāgarājārūpan darśyati / yathā yathā sattvavinayaṃ tathā tathā rū-
4.	paṃ darśayati / eṣa laukikīsādhana lakṣajāpataḥ // lokottaralakṣajāpena daśasu dikṣu sarvabuddhakṣetraṃ sarvatathāgatasammukhadarśanaṃ paśyati / sarvatathāgatavyākaraṇaṃ pratilabhate / avaivartikam adhiṣṭhānam adhiṣṭhito bhaviṣyati / yāvad bodhimaṇḍalaparyavasānād iti // mudrayā gṛhīta-
5.	sya baddhaparyaṅkam eva ca / sarvasattvadayābhūtamaitracittakaruṇātmakaḥ / amoghapāśahṛdayam ekavāraṃ japiṣyati / saha yā(jā)pamātrasya aṣṭāsītijāpakṛtāni bhavanti sarvatathāgatā samāśvāsayanti / smṛtibuddhi ojena śarire prakṣapsyanti / padyajāpamātrayā ṣaṭpāramitāpa-
6.	ripūri bhaviṣyati tañ ca duṣyapaṭaṃ rūparaśmarayo niścaranti / āryāvalokiteśvaraṃ pariśuddhenātmabhāvena svapnadarśan dāśyanti / sarvavarāṇi anupradāśyati / sarvakarmāvaraṇāni vāsya pariśuddhāni bhaviṣyanti / sarvapāpāvaraṇāni parikṣayaṃ gacchati paryādānaṃ vāntībhavati / sarvavyādhayaḥ sarvalaukikīkārya-
7.	sādhanāni paramasiddhir bhaviṣyati / buddhānusmṛtir bhāvayitavyeti // samāñjalīdṛḍhīkṛtyaḥ kuñcitāgryā mukhāsthitā / kaniṣṭābhyān tu vikacā madhyamā padmā sannibhāḥ / mantram etām udāharaḥ / [96] oṃ turu turu padme amoghasiddhiṃ hūṃ // amoghasiddhir idaṃ mudrā sarvakarmeṣu siddhimām // asya pravartamānasya
14b (P23a3, D.24a4)
1.	śīghraṃ sidhyati sarvavit / darśanan dāśyate śīghraṃ amoghasiddhiparamadurlabhaṃ sarvavaraṃ pracāreti / laukikīlokottaras tathā / sarvapāpād dhi mucyeti puṇyarāśi vivardhate nityaṃ maṅgalyaśabdaṃ pravartate nityaṃ rakṣanti devā iti // // vajrabandhaṃ subandhañ ca dṛḍhacitta sadā bhavaḥ / hṛdayan tu prasārayaṃ tarjanyā tu nikuñcayaṃ / amoghapadmena vi-
2.	khyātā padmamaṇḍalamudrāyā hṛdayaṃ padmeśvaraṃ mudrā sarvakarmārthasādhikā mantram etam udīryan tu tata amogharājam udīrayam // [97] oṃ amoghahṛdayapadme dhara dhara padmadhara mahāmaṇḍalahṛdaya hūṃ // eṣa mudrā pravartate hṛ- // // dayaṃ padmadharasya tu // amoghahṛdayasiddhaṃ vai tattvabhūta na sansayaḥ / sarvatra sarvakarmāṇi sidhyante nātra śaṃ-
3.	sayaḥ / guhya<ṃ>mudrā vāryeta suguhya<ṃ>mantrajāpataḥ / susnātaṃ śucivastrāṇi śuddhacittañ ca dharayaḥ / sarvasattvadayābhūtaḥ / gurubhakti sadā bhavah / prasādaparamo nityaṃ padmapāṇi ca rato bhavaḥ / śīghraṃ sidhyati karmāṇi śīghraṃ buddhatvam āpnuyāt / śīghraṃ lokeśvaram avāpnoti / śīghraṃ sarvasattvahitānvitaṃ bhave-
4.	d iti // // vāmabāhuprasāryamātran tu mukhato padmākārasaṃsthitaḥ parivartya karer ūrdhvamātrā anāmikā jyeṣṭhāṅguli kuñcayaṃ prasāryadakṣiṇam bāhūṃ aṅgulyā padma(dmā)kāra- maṇḍalaṃ nikucya jyeṣṭhāṅgulimadhyamāpāsahasto mahāmoghaśāntanetro maitracittaś ca sarvasattvakarū(ru)ṇātmakaḥ / mantram etām udā-
5.	haraḥ // [98] oṃ paśupatipāśadharaḥ śatasahasraraśmipratimaṇḍitakāya ākarṣaṇi bodhani saṃbodhani bhuru bhuru amoghapāsasiddhe hūṃ // etā mudrā mantrā pravarteta vārāṃ ekaviṃśati / tata amoghapāsahṛdayaṃ pravartayan dakṣiṇahaste pāsā(sa)mudrāyā kā(ka)ra kṣepayaṃ puna svasthāna dhāraya saptavārā ma-
6.	ntra pravarteta saptavārāṃ ca kṣepayaṃ / amogharājaṃ pravarteta<ḥ> śuddhāśayaḥ para saṃ<m>bhavet / saha pravartanamātre tu pāsamudrāmahāmogha sarvabodhyākarṣaṇaṃ sadya budhatva sidhyati / anyāni vividhakāryāṇi yathā manasi cintayaṃ sadyam ākarṣayate sarvaṃ sadya siddhir anuttaram / devanāgā ca yakṣā ca gandharvāsuragaruḍakinna-
7.	ramahoragāḥ sarve sadya samāyānti darśanād vidyādharasya ca / daśadikṣu sarvabuddhā vyavalokayanti darśana<ṃ>n dāśyanti tattvataḥ / bodhisatvā samāyānti vandanārthe vidyādharasya tu āryāvalokiteśvaravaradanātha mūddhni pāṇiñ ca sthāpayiṣyanti / pariśuddhenātmabhāvena sadyan dāsyati darśanaṃ / yadi vīcīgataya
15a (P23b8, D.25a3)
1.	sattvā pañcānantaryakāraka āryapavādakā ye ca ye ca saddharmakṣepakāḥ / darśanā mudrām etan tu grahaṇādipravartanaṃ sadyaṃ viśaṭhanti sarvapāpāni darśanaṃ sadyaṃ dāśyanti nāyakaḥ sarvakāryāṇi sidhyanti mudrāgrahaṇamātrayā // asya mudrādarśanamātrasya mantrapravartamānasya vidyādharadarśanasya tulyam etāṃ / āryāvarokiteśvarasya <m>mukhan darśa-
2.	nan draṣṭavyaḥ / janmaparaparakaipa śatasahasrasañcita pāpakarmāvaraṇaṃ mudrāgrahaṇadarśanamātrasya sarvam eti(ta)d dhi śudhyante nātra sansaya iti // samāṅguli dṛḍhīkṛtya padmākāran tu paṇḍitaḥ / tarjanyā maṇīkṛtya madhyamamātrā nikuñcaya mahācintāmaṇidhvajāgran tu ūrdhva bāhūṃ prasārayam / abhiṣeka śire dadyān mantram etām udāharam // [99] oṃ stryadhvānuga-

4.	ddhir bhaviṣyati / sarvalokaikam ūrdhasthāyī bhaviṣyatīti // vajrapadmabandhaśiraṃ mūrdhne prasāryāṅgulimukhaiḥ sadā / tarjanyāṃ aṅkuśākāra svamukhena bhṛkuṭīmukhaṃ krodharājamudrā sarvakarmaprasādhanaṃ / sarvasiddhikaraṃ mudrā sarvasādhanam uttamam / mantram etām anusmārya sarvasādhanam uttamam // [100] oṃ krodharājamahāmo-
5.	gha hana hana sarvaduṣṭāṃ hūṃ eṣā smāritamātrāyā mudrā // // mantramahābalaḥ / viśiṭhanti sarvaduṣṭvā vai sumeruṃ capi viśīryate / mudrāyā gṛhyamānāyāḥ sarve viśaṭhanti ca pāpakā duṣṭasatvā duṣṭanāgāś ca duṣṭavighnavināyakāḥ sarve te prasamaṃ yānti mudrāyā grahamātrayāh sarve vasagatā tiṣṭhanti devāye ca mānu-
6.	ṣāśramaṇā brāhmaṇā caiva sūdrā kṣatriyam eva ca / sarve vasagatā nityam tiṣṭhanasa ayam iti // samāhitaḥ sadā sandhāya samāṅguṣṭhasamuktitā / madhyamā madhyamā caiva ūrdhvamātra nikuñcayet // eṣa mahāpadmatārādevyā mudrām uttama sarvatra siddhir anuttarām iti // kudmālānyasamāpadmā prasāryāṃguṣṭhareva ca kanyasāmātra kuñceta mahāśvetā
7.	divyaṃ mudrā sarvasiddhir anuttarāḥ // // vajrākārakṛtāṃ jalyā dṛḍhabandhena dakṣiṇakarāveṣṭaye prajñāḥ / mukham vai bhṛkuṭī tathā / bhṛkuṭāmahāmudrā sarvakāmapradāyikā // // añjalīkaradvaya prasāryan tu gujyañ cātra prasaryo karamadhye nikuñcayet // kanyasīprasāryas tu śaṃkalīpadmākāra hṛdisthānas tu sthāpayan / eṣa mudrā
15b (P24b4, D.26a1)
1.	mahāmudrā sarveṣām maṇḍalanivāsina devatām eṣa mudrā pravarteta sarve tuṣyanti maṇḍaladevatā // sarve samaprāsamāyāntu sa[r]ve rakṣā kariṣyanti na sa[ṃ]śaya iti // eṣa ca mudrā prasāritapāṇi añjalīpadmākāramukhaṃ kṛtvā hṛdisthānañ ca sthāpayaṃ dūtināṃ yakṣiṇināñ ca mudreyaṃ saṃprakīrtita // // vajramuṣti subandhāsyaṃ kuñcayitvā tu madhyame / śeṣāṅguṣṭhani-
2.	hite padmākārā tu mukhe smṛtā / saptatārādiyaṃ mudrā sarvatra siddhir anuttarā // sarve viśaṭhanti pāpakā vinaśyanti sarvakleśāni sarvavyādhi na saṃśayaḥ sarvakalpaprasiddhas tu amoghasiddhir uttamaḥ // // karadvaya samaṃ sthāpya vajrāka(kā)rapadma bandhayaṃ tarjanīyaṃ kuñcamej jyeṣṭhāṅguṣṭāni pīdrayet / hayagrīvam iyaṃ mudrāḥ sarvakaloṣur uttamā
3.	padmākāra añjalī samādhāya tarjanī gratthiṣu madhyamā kuṃśānāma ekam etu prasārayam / amoghapāsam idaṃ mudrā vaḍavāmukha<ṃ>m etan tu bhakṣayed vajrabhāṣitā sarvakarmakarā eṣā sarvasiddhir anuttarād iti // // eṣa mudrā tarjanyāṅguṣṭhasaṃkalā śeṣā nikuṃcya padmākāra hṛdisthānañ ca sthāpaye nīlakaṇṭham iyaṃ
4.	mudrā sarvakalpaviśāradāḥ / padmamuṣṭidvayopāṇi vāmadakṣiṇasthāpayaṃ saumyavadanam āsthāpya maitrāyānayanadvayā ekādaśamukhaṃ mudrā sarvatrā<a>pratihatā tathā // samaṃ samāṅguṣṭha saṃkocāṅgulimukhaṃ padmākāra dvayo tarjanyā mudreyaṃ īśvarā viddhaḥ // eṣaiva mudrā madhyamā candrākāra kuñcayam //
5.	tarjanyā vāma prasārayaṃ maheśvaramudrā sarvabhūtapradārśanam // // śaṃkalī dṛḍhīkṛtya dvayapāṇi prasārayaṃ / punaḥ hṛdi saṃsthāpya mudreyaṃ yama sadā // vāmapāṇi prasāryan tu ūrdhamātrāṅguli nikuñcayet / dakṣiṇaṃ hṛdi sthānya jyeṣṭhāṅgulikanyasi kuñcayet / śeṣan tu prasārayaṃ varuṇeyaṃ mahāmudrā sarvanāgā-
6.	karṣaṇaṃ prabhuḥ // // dvayopāṇi dṛḍhīkṛtya viparītāṅguli veṣṭayaṃ / dakṣiṇavāmādyāṃ hṛdi sthāna kuñca sthāpayam / kuberākhyam iyaṃ mudrā sarvayakṣavidāraṇaṃ // // dakṣiṇa ūrdhvapāṇiñ ca tarjanījyeṣṭhāṅguli nikuñcayam / vāmapāṇi prasāryan tu aṅgulyā ūrdhva prasārayam // eṣa brahmānviṣaṃ mudrā sarvatra karmasādhakā // //
7.	eṣaiva mudrayā dakṣiṇāṃ kamyasāṅgulya kuñcayam / eṣa riṣigaṇā mudrā sarvadevanamaskṛtyā // // dvayapāṇi dṛḍhībandhya sudarśanaṃ cakramudramā / viṣṇumudrām iyaṃ proktā sarvāsuraghātanam // // dakṣiṇaṃ bījapuram vāmaṃ triśūlākṛti kuru / jyeṣṭhākanyasī tu nikuñcayaṃ mudreyaṃ rudram ākhyātaṃ sarvabhūtavidāraṇam / dakṣiṇa-
16a (P.25b1 , D.26b6)
1.	muṣṭibandhena tarjanyā tu prasārayaṃ vāmataḥ kaṭisthāna muṣṭiṃ [ba]dhvā tu sthāpaya[n] tarya(rja)nvām prasāryan tu raudradṛṣṭī sadā kuru kumārākhyām iyaṃ mudrā devasenāpatis tathā // // dvayapāṇi prasārya ūrdhvan tu trayaṅgulyā prasāryayaṃ tarjanyā jyeṣṭhāṅguṣṭhaṃ nipīḍayam / ādityam iyaṃ mudrā sahasraraśmipramuñcanaḥ // // eṣaiva mudrā trayoṅgulyā nipīḍayaṃ dve dve
2.	prasārayaṃ somākhyām iyaṃ mudrā tārāgaṇavirājitaḥ // tatre mānimahāmantrapadāni bhavanti / sarvakarmārthasādhanasādhikāḥ sarvapāpaharāśvaiva sarvakleśavināśakāḥ / sarvāvaraṇaviśuddhyarthaṃ sarvakarmakṣayaṃ karam / amoghasiddhi siddhyante amoghakarmasādhakaḥ //[101] oṃ padmatāre turu turu hūṃ //[102] oṃ mahāpadme

4.	nilakaṇṭheśvara bhuru bhuru hūṃ // [111] oṃ somyavadane prasidaśāsaya hūṃ // [112] oṃ padmeśvari sādhaya hūṃ // [113] oṃ amogheśvari hūṃ // [114] oṃ samari tare tare hūṃ // [115] oṃ nāgapadmani hūṃ // [116] oṃ yakṣādhipati muru muru hūṃ //[117] oṃ amoghe brahmārate hūṃ // [118] oṃ riṣivarade amoghabhuje hūṃ // [119] oṃ amoghaviṣṇurājapadmanābhe sarasamantabhu-
5.	je svāhā // [120] oṃ rudra tuṭi mili svāhā // [121] oṃ śaktidhare amoghavati svāhā // [122] oṃ sahasrakiraṇe hūṃ turi svāhā //[123] oṃ somaprabhe hūṃ // uktamudrāmudramantrās tathaiva ca // pṛthag mudrāṃ pṛthag māntrāṃ sādhaya vidyādharaṃ sadā // yathoktā sarvakarmāṇi kuryā amāsataḥ śucisaucasamācārasusnātaśucivastrā-
6.	ṇi dhārakaḥ / sugandhagandhānuliptām bāhupāṇilepayamāna ekaikamudrā pravarteta / ekaikaṃ mudrā pravartayaṃ sapravartitamudrāś ca mantrāsmaraṇena ca / koṭikoṭiśatavārasahasrāṇi mudrābhon tripravartitā // mudrāyā pravartayamānāyā mahāpuṇyaskandhā samanvāgato bhaviṣyati / sarvabodhisattvavyākṛtādhiṣṭhito bhaviṣya-
7.	ti / sarvadevatā saṃrakṣito bhaviṣyati / sarvalokyakapūjyo bhaviṣyati / sarvasattvaikaguruko bhavati / sarvamaṇḍanamudrākalpapaṭalasamayam anupraviṣṭā bhavati / aparamitāyuṣaś ca bhaviṣyati / amoghasiddhividyādharaś ca bhaviṣyati / ādeyavacanaś ca bhaviṣyati / smṛtibuddhiviśāradaś ca bhaviṣyati / apratihata-
16b (P26a5, D.27b4)
1. buddhiś ca bhaviṣyati / āryāvalokiteśvaravarado bhaviṣyati / nityaṃ darśanakāmatā bhaviṣyati / nityaṃ ca varapradāyako bhaviṣyati / nityaṃ śakrapratyarthikapratyāmitrasarvaduṣṭapraduṣṭāś ca parājito bhaviṣyati / nityaṃ sarvatra jayado bhaviṣyati / sarvayakṣarākṣasabhūtapisācavighnavināyakāsuranāgāś ca darśanīyo bhavati / sarvāryāmahāvi-
2.	śārado bhavati / sarvarājarājāmātyaśāntaḥ pura parivārāvasagatā tiṣṭhanti sarvatraikamūrtir bhaviṣyati / sarvatraikagurusthānīś ca bhaviṣyati / dīrghāyuṣaś ca bhavati / sarvavyādhivinirmuktāś ca bhaviṣyati / sarvadoṣamalamātsaryerṣyāpagato bhaviṣyati / susmṛtisupariśuddhakāyaś ca bhaviṣyati / maraṇakāle ca mahātathāgatadarśana
2.	pratilabhate / athāryāvalokiteśvaradarśanan dāśyati / sarve te samāśvāsayati / ehi putraka sukhāvatīlokadhātum anuvrajasva / sūpasthitasmṛtikāle kariṣyati sarvanarakadvārāṇi pithitāni bhaviṣyati / sarvadurgatigamanāparimukto bhavati / eṣa paścimakagarbhāvāsajātyā bhaviṣyati / nityañ ca jāti-
3.	smaratā pratilabhate sarvatra upapādukapadmebhyā prajāsyate / buddhakṣetrād buddhakṣetraṃ sa kramiṣyati yāvad bodhimaṇḍaparyavasānād iti //

16b4(P.26b3, D.28a3)

23a (P37b8, D.39b6)

1.	ryāvalokiteśvarahṛdayam / asya dhāraṇi dine dine trin vārā sandhyā anusmariṣyati / tena te vidyādharasya-s tryadhvānugatāḥ sarṣapamaṇḍalā ārādhitāni bhaviṣyanti / pūjitamānitāś ca bhaviṣyanti / mahāpuṇyaskandhasambhāramahākuśalamūlāvaropito bhaviṣyati / sarvve te tathāgatas tryadhvānugatā sarṣapamaṇḍalā vidyādharasya smṛtibuddhibalaṃ vīryaojaṃ prakṣepsyanti / dine dine sarvvata-
2.	thāgatadarśāvino bhaviṣyanti / nityañ ca tathāgatā samanvāhṛto bhaviṣyanti / āryāvalokiteśvaranityānubaddho bhaviṣyanti / nitya<ṃ>ñ ca darśanavaradāyako bhavati / dṛṣṭeva dharme sāṃsārikāni duḥkhabhayabhairavāni bhayabhītāni praśamiṣyanti / na ca punar vyādhiṣyati / na jvaran na viṣan na garaṃ yogā na śastraṃ nāgni nodakaṃ nāśiniśarīraṃ krāmiṣyati / na śatrubhayaṃ bhaviṣyati na paracakra-
3.	bhayaṃ na parasainyabhayan na pratyarthikapratyāmitrabhayan na devabhayaṃ na yakṣagandharvvāsuragaruḍakinnaramahoragarākṣasaḍākiniñ ca kāmarūpi yoginī bhaviṣyati / na ca yojana(nā)vyantareṇa na śakyaṃ sandhārayituṃ dūratā prapalāyante / na ca vighnāni cāsya na bhaviṣyanti / yaś ca kasyacid avicikasthāniyā satvā bhavet pañcānantaryakārakaḥ / āryāpavādakaḥ sa-
4.	ddharmapratikṣepakaḥ kalpaśatasahasrasañcitakarmāvaraṇapariveṣṭitaśarīraṃ tasyāpi dṛṣṭeva dharme sarvve niravaśeṣaṃ paryādānaṅ gacchati parimucyate / yadi aṣṭottarasahasram anāpala(lapi)tajāpo dāśyati / ekenāpi upavāse jāpena japitavyaḥ sa janmakoṭiśatasahasrakalpāni samsārād vinivarttate dhāraṇījāpa karmmāṇi parimucyate
5.	pūrvvimakā[rmā(?)]kalpasahasrasañcitaṃ vinasyanti pāpāni na bhūya samsāradoṣā liṣya(pya)te / padmam iva anopalipyate / padmam iva anopalipto bhavati / sarvvapāpāvaraṇebhyaḥ pañcabhūmi da(dṛ)ṣṭadharmeṣu pratilabhate / sevamānayā jāpavidhiniyāmasthāya avaivarttikasthānaṃ pratilabhate / smaraṇamātreṇa ṣaṭpāramitāṃ paripūrṇṇo bhavi-
6.	ṣyati daśapāramitānugato bhaviṣyati / dine dine aṣṭottaraśatajāpena dṛṣṭadharmikasamantabhadratvaṃ pratilabhate / sarvvamantrakalpamaṇḍalaṃ mudrānupraviṣṭo bhavati / sarvvasamayapraviṣṭāṃ sarvvasamudayasamanubaddho bhava(vi)ṣyati / vidyādharasya darśanamātrayā avicī(vīci)kasaṃsthāniyāni pāpāni vinaśyanti / antaśa śmāśānam api vidyādharo praviṣṭo bhavati / tañ ca mṛtakāṃsthi(sthiṃ) mṛtakamāmsaṃ vā mṛtakakuṇapam vā
7.	antaśa mṛtakatailanirmālyakuḍyam vā vidyādharasya cchāyānipātaṃ<m> bhaviṣyati / yatra yatra sa satvāḥ / narakaparāyaṇaṃ bhavati / tatra tatra tatkṣaṇāt parimucyate sukhāvatilokadhātur utpatsyate / nātra kāṃkṣā na va(vi)matir ū(u)tpādayitavyam / kiṃ puna ye bahutara·adhikāraṃ kariṣyati / yaḥ kaścit kulaputro vā kuladuhitā vā bhikṣubhikṣuṇyopāsakopāsikā vā
28a (P47b8, D.49b3)
1.	śraddhānusāriṇā vā ayan dhāraṇīn dhārayati / vācayati / paṭhati / svādhyāyati / cintayiṣyanti / bhāvayiṣyati pūjayiṣyati / likhiṣyati likhāpayiṣyati / sa ca niyata·avaivarttiko bhaviṣyati anuttarāyāṃ samyaksa[ṃ]bodhau / dṛṣṭeva dharme mahati mahati mahāguṇaśatasahasraṃ pratilabhave(te) / anayaiva dhāriṇyā dṛṣṭeva dharme ca sarvvakuśalamūlasambhāraṣaṭpāramitām anuyuktakuśalamūla-

28b (P49a1, D.50b4)
1.	travādam vā nidhivādam vā dhānyavādam vā kanyālābham vā karmmasiddhiṃ vā mantrasiddhim vā vaśīkaraṇam vā aiśvaryalābham vā tat sarvvam vā maṇinā madhyaṃ dṛśyate śubham aśubhaṃ<m> vā sarvvam etat<a> tad dṛśyate / varṣam vā avarṣam vā / upadrava upasarggaītayo maramahāmarapaśumaragorama(mara)aśvamarahastimaram vā mahābhayabhītam vā svacakraparacakram vā tat sarvvaṃ yathā darśamaṇḍalan tathā dṛśyate / yaṃ cintayati yaṃ prārthayati si-

30a (P52a4, D.53b5)
1. 	sram amoghaṛājajaptena tāḍayaṃ tato nāgapāśaṃ gṛhya yasyāṃ diśi rājagṛhe tatra viśeṣā pāśa bhrāmaya rājānaṃ śāntaḥpuraparivāraṃ vaśyā bhaviṣyanti / yāvajjīvena tiṣṭhanti / saṃgrāme yudhyamāne krodharājaṃ japatā pāśaṃ saṃgrāmamadhye kṣeptavyam / mahāntaṃ āśīviṣaṃ prādurbhavati / vikaṭākṣaṃ lolajihvaṃ aṅgāradīptinayanaṃ saha darśanamātrāṇi caturaṅgaṃ balakāyadiśividiśāni

34a (P.60a3, D.61b6)

35a ( P62a1 , D.63b6)
1.	kapātramadhya tiṣṭhati tāvad daśasu dikṣu sarvvatathāgatānāṃ purataḥ / nānārasarasāgratā divyamahāratā prādurbhavanti saśrāvakabodhisatvagaṇā mahāparṣanmaṇḍale santarpitāni bhaviṣyanti / sarvve ca te tathāgatā te ca bodhisatvagaṇā saśrāvakaśa<ṃ>ṅghā tasya vidyādharasya dakṣiṇā śodhayanti / modakam aṣṭottaravārā sataṃ parijapya krodharājenābhiṣiṃcya aṃkuśīrāje udakaṃ

3.	si[kāra]kāmatā pravarttate / vidyādhareṇa yathā manasābhiprāyaṃ varaṃ vaktavyaṃ<ḥ> sa ca sarvvan dadāti agnidevatā vaśagatām upatiṣṭhanti / sarvvakāryasiddhim anuprayacchati / agniskandhamadhye sahasrajvālārcciyet himavat śītalaṃ praviśati nirgacchati pānīyavata(t) hastena agninā parimā(mo)kṣamaṇḍaleti jvalantaṃ mūrddhā śire sthāpayati na ca ekavālam api dahati na cāgnivaśa ga-
4.	cchati / mukhena jvalantaṃ dhārayati na ca kiñcid uṣṇam api śarīre nipatati / yathā candanaśītalaṃ prahlādayati anyāni vividhakāryāṇi kariṣyati / vastrānte badhnīyā na dahyati / jalamadhye sthāpya na niṣamyate / sarvvahomavidhinā svarūpeṇa-m upatiṣṭhati / sarvvatra kārya niyokṣata tatra tatra sarvvakāryasiddhim anuprayacchati / sarvvatra mantramaṇḍalasiddhikalpam upadiśati

35b (P62b8, D.64b7)
1.	rmakārayam // sarṣapaṃ ghṛtaṃ dadhimadhulājam āhutī / divyatāmraṃ palaṃ trīṇi dviguṇaṃ hiṅgulam eva ca kiṃ kuṣṭhaṃ // palañ caiva śvetakāñ ca candanapalo daśa manacchilāpalaṃ bhavet / etā dravyasaṃyuktā dviguṇaṃ haritālaṃ tathā / mūṣāyāṃ pūrayitvā kho(khā)trāgnimadhya nidhāpayet / juhuyād aṣṭasahasrāṇi suvyaktaṃ susamāhitaṃ śuciśaucasamācāravastradhārakasusnātasugandhagandhādhivāsitaḥ / nānārasabaliṃ sthāpya puṣpaphalam eva
2.	ca pūrṇṇe aṣṭasahasrāṇi jāpam āhutim eva ca / tato nivedyabalipuṣpadhūpañ ca arghapādya nidāpayaṃ / kṣīreṇa niśāmyatā agnikṣareṇa mūṣā niśāmyatā / tato vidyādhareṇa aṣṭottarasahasrajaptayā sarṣapaṃ<r> āhantavyam / krodharājena ekaviṃśativārā abhiṣektavyaṃ / tato mūṣā jvalati / tato vidyādhareṇa taṃ mūṣā pūjayitavyan tato tāmrasamudgako vā rūpyasa[mu]dgake vā taṃ maṇi mūṣāyāṃ /
3.	gṛhya tatra samudgake sthāpayitavyam / yathā cintāmaṇi yathāyam amoghajvālaṃ mahāmahānubhāvo bhaviṣyati / rātrau candrāvabhāsaṃ darśayati / divase sūryāvabhāsaṃ darśayati / yadā pākakāle taṃ maṇi sthāpya sarvva.arthana<pa>yanapākaṃ sādhayati / yadā homaṃ kuryāt tadā khātramadhye sthāpayam āhutiṃ juhūyāt mahatā agnijvālaṃ prādurbhavati / sarvvatra sarvvakāryāṇi pratīya(dīpa)kṛ-
4.	tyaṃ kariṣyati / mahāyuddhasaṃgrāmamadhye sūrapuruṣasya susnātaśucivastradhāriṇā sannaddhavaddhakavacena taṃ mahāmaṇim ūrddhaśire sthāpayitavyam / ekākinā saṃgrāmam avattaret tataḥ sa puruṣaḥ / ekāgnijvālī bhūto bhaviṣyati / tato yuddhasaṃgrāmaparacakre nirjjitā bhaviṣyaṃti / daśavidiśāni vihvalī prapalā(pāla)yante / na ca bhūya kadācit paracakraśa-
5.	trubhayaṃ bhaviṣyati / na ca pratyarthikapratyā(tya)mitrabhayaṃ bhaviṣyati maṇinā hastena gṛhya bilabhavane praveṣṭavya sarvvabhavanadvārā apāvṛtāni tiṣṭhanti sarvvabhavananivāsinā agratam upatiṣṭhanti / nāgabhavanaṃ sarvve gatvā taṃ maṇi haste gṛhya bhavanamadhye praveṣṭavyaṃ sarvvam udakaṃ na bhūyaḥ prabhaviṣyati / tataḥ sarvvanāgāḥ agratam upatiṣṭhaṃti / satyapratijñe-
6.	na tiṣṭhaṃti / vaśagatā yāvajjīva karmakarā bhaviṣyanti / ājñākarāvatiṣṭhaṃti / sarvvasasyatṛṇagulmavanaspato sukhena varddhayanti / mṛdusnigdhakomalā bhaviṣyanti / ojavanta smṛtivantaś ca alpābādhaś ca bhaviṣyanti / ye ca te sarvvasatvā taṃ sasyaṃ puṣpaphalā bhokṣyanti / anyāni bahūni bahūtarakāryāṇi kariṣyanti mahāmaṇi amoghajvālamahāgnijvālam iti // ato amoghaprabhāsavilo-
7.	kitākarṣaṇam āryāvalokiteśvarasya svarūpeṇa draṣṭukāmena / tato vidyādhareṇa śucinā śucivastradhāriṇā bhavitavyaṃ / susnātena gandhodakādhivāsitaśarīreṇa / tato arkkakāṣṭhasamidbhir agni prajvālayitavyam / sarṣapa.agara(ru)candanadadhimadhughṛtāktāṃ ghṛta(aṣta)sahasram juhūyāt tato amoghāṃkuśena gugguluṃ / ekavi[ṃ]śāhutayo juhūyāt / āryāvalokiteśva[raḥ sva]rūpeṇa agratam upa-
36a (P63b8, D.65b7)
1.	tiṣṭhati / sarvvābhiprāyavarāṇi paripūrayati / yathābhilaṣitamanasikārāṃ varāṃ dadāti / tato vidyādhareṇa yathābhilaṣitamanasikāreṇa varaṃ brūyāt tato āryāvalokiteśvarasya sarvvan dadāti // tato añjanaṃ sādhayitukāmena tena vidyādhareṇa śucinā śucivastradhāriṇā susnātena bhavitavyaṃ / ekarātropavustena tāmrasamudgake kṛtvā tataḥ sovīrāñjanena dviguṇaharitālamisre-

36b (P.64b7, D.66b7)

37a (P65b7, D.67b7)

37b (P66b7, D.69a1)
1.	rocanam āsādya mahālohitam eva ca / kuṃkumaṃ ca samāyojya pūrayet tāmrabhājanam amoghapāśapratimasyāgrataḥ / aṣṭasahasrapūrṇṇaṃ parijapya cchīnavastrāṇi cchādayaṃ / tato khadirakāṣṭham arkakāṣṭhasamidbhir agni prajvālya lājāsarṣapaguggulata<ṃ>ṇḍulatilamiśritaṃ dadhimadhughṛtāktāṃ aṣṭottaravārā sahasraṃ yujūyāt pūrṇṇe aṣṭottarasahasran tato agniniśāntā sūkṣmaśāntibhasmanā gṛhya tatra bhājana kṣeptavyaḥ /
2.	tato punar aṣṭottaraśatavārā japya krodharājenābhiṣiṃcya mudrayā ca(cā)bhiṣiktam mudrayam / tato ūrṇṇākeśatilakaṃ kuru ākāśam utpatati / mahāvidyādharaṃ cakravarttarājā bhaviṣyati / sarvvavidyādharāṇāṃ prabhuḥ / ākuñcitakuṇḍalakeśaś ca bhaviṣyati / navanavativarṣasahasrāṇī jīvati / sarvvabhavanadvārāṇi apāvṛtā nityam eva tiṣṭhaṃti / lalāṭatilakaṃ kṛtvā antarddhito bhavati / sarvvatra bhava-
3.	nāni praviśati / sarvvatra bhavane ādhipatir bhaviṣyati sarvvabhūtayakṣarākṣasabhavanāni apāvṛtadvārāṇi tiṣṭhanti / sarvvabhūtayakṣarākṣasā sabalaparivārāgatām upatiṣṭhanti / yāvajjīva kiṃkarā bhaviṣyaṃti / akṣīṇy añjayed rātrau yathā divyaṃ paśyati / sarvvatra mahānidhānaṃ paśyati / dharaṇi-r-vedhavalapātāladvāra.apāvṛtaṃ paśyati / pātālabhavanaṃ paśyati
4.	sarvvapātālakāryāṇi kṛtvā punar api nirgacchati / mahānadī- jalamadhye praviśati / yathā gṛhavimānaṃ paśyati / saptaratnavibhūṣitavimānaṃ paśyati / ye ca tatra jalavāśināḥ sarvve vaśagatā tiṣṭhanti / nāgaśarabhavane yathā svagṛhaṃ praviśati nirgacchati / sakalaśarīraṃ samāla(li)peta yuddhasaṃgrāmamadhya praviśat sannaddhavaddhakavaco dṛśyate sarvvatra jayado
5.	bhaviṣyati / mukhāgre dhārayaṃ rājakule praviśeta // sāntaḥpuraparivāraṃ vaśagatā tiṣṭhanti / yaṃ vācā vadati tat sarvvaṃ kariṣyati / yaṃ yācayasi tat sarvvan dāsyanti / yam ājñāpayasi tat sarvvam ājñā kariṣyanti / mukhaṃ parimā[r]jya yatra yatraiva gacchati tatra tatra strīpuruṣadārakadārikā vaśagatām upatiṣṭhanti / ātmanena dhanena vā / dakṣiṇapāṇau lepayet
6.	 yasya spṛśati sa vasyo bhaviṣyati / dhanadhānyahiraṇyadāsadasīkarmakarapauruṣeyāṇi vaśagatā kiṃkā(ka)rakā tiṣṭhanti / dakṣiṇatarjanyāṅgulyo lepayaṃ aśini.abhrakāle garjamānaṃ abhram abhimukhaṃ sthitvā tarjayaṃ / sarvvaśītavātāśini praśamayanti / yojanasamantena-n ākramanti / ativarṣa dakṣiṇahaste abhram abhimukha<ṃ>n darśa<ye>yati varṣa prasamayati / anāvṛṣṭikāle dakṣiṇakaratale
7.	lepanaṃ nāgabhavanasare udakaṃ trīṇi vārā capeṭan trātavyan tatkṣaṇan nāgarājā mahāvarṣadhārān utsṛjati yadi kṣaṇe na varṣayati tadā pañca vārā hantavyaḥ / tatsarvvanāgā varṣadhārān utsṛjaṃti / yadi na varṣanti / tadā sarvve nāgā śīrṣarogā prādurbhavanti / yadi sapta vārā āhane sarvvanāgarājānau anayā(ya)vyasanam āpadyante / sarvvanāgarājānau kulotsādanakṛ<ṃ>taṃ bhaviṣyati / grahagṛhītānā
38a (P67b7. D.70a1)
1.	sarvveṣāṃ akṣīṇy añjayec caturāśitigrahasahasrāṇi añjitamātreṇa praśamayanti / jvaritānāṃ lalāṭan tilakaṃ kṛtvā sarvvajvarai ekāhikadvaitīyakatraitīyakacāturthakajvarā praśamayanti / sarvvavyādhispṛṣṭānāṃ snapanaṃ sarvvavyādhinā sadya parimucyate / <ś>citrakuṣṭhavicarccikapāmapiṭakadadrū parimucyate / sarvvagaṇḍalohaliṅgabhagandaravisarpagaṇḍamālamālādilepamātreṇa parimucyate / sarvvaviṣadaṣṭānāṃ li-
2.	ptamātreṇa nirvviṣo bhavati / sarvvaviṣamantrasya pītamātreṇa parimucyate / sarvvakukṣirogiṇāṃ gulmina jalodaraplīhapārśvasūlahṛdacchūlavastiśūla.arṣamatī(sati)sārapramehavisūcikājīrṇṇaudāvartta uṣṇodaka saha pītamātre praśamiṣyanti / na ca bhūyaḥ kadācit prakupyanti / mūrddhagata kṛtvā rocana(nā)guṭikāṃ vivaradvāre samīpa sthitvā dakṣiṇakare lipya dvārā capeṭān dātavyaḥ sarvvabi-
3.	ladvārā apāvṛtā bhavanti / nirbhaya praveṣṭavya na bhūya kiñcid vighnāni bhaviṣyanti / ye ca tatra bhavananivāsinā te sarvve agrata vaśagatā bhavanti / sarvvakāryasādhanamahāsiddhir bhaviṣyati / dvau caraṇau lipya agnijvālamadhye praviśed agnidevatā svarūpeṇa agratam upatiṣṭhati hastaṃ gṛhnāti / vidyādharasya kathayati praviśa vidyādharasya madīyabhavanavimānaṃ ājñā-
4.	paya vidyādha[ra] sarvakāryam iti karṣyāmi / tata vidyādhare kariṣyatīti / dvayabāhau limpet saptanāgabalo bhaviṣyati / dvayam ūruṃ limpet / anihatamallo bhavati / jaṃghaṃ lepayet mahānadīgādhaṃ labhate pāram uttarati / kaṇṭhe limpra(mpya) vanavivare praviśya vanavivarabhavanadvāra.apāvṛtā bhaviṣyanti / sarvvadravyauṣadhaya svarūpeṇa agratam upatiṣṭhanti / yam icchati
5.	taṃ grahāya / mukhāgra rocanaguṭikāṃ gṛhya girikandaragahvarāṇi praviśya uccasukrośaṃ trīṇi vārā karttavyaḥ / ye tatra girigahvarakandareṣu bhavanaguhyadvā[ra.]apāvṛtā bhavanti / ye ca tatra nivāsinān tana sarvva svakasvakarūpaṇa agratam upatiṣṭhanti / sarvvakarmakarā bhavanti / ye ca tatra tṛṇagulmavanaspatidravyau[ṣa]dhaya svarūpeṇa agratam upasthā-
6.	syanti / ye ca rasavarā te sravanti / te ca rasaparipālakā svarūpeṇa agratam upatiṣṭhanti / sarvva vaśagatā bhaviṣyanti / yatheṣṭaṃ nirbhayaṃ sarvvakāryāṇi karttavyam iti / hṛdayaṃ tilakaṃ kṛtvā<ṃ> yat kiñci[d] hṛdgatāni / āśāmanorathacintābhiprāyakāryāṃ tatkṣaṇe sarvvaṃ sidhyatīti / udaraṃ tilakaṃ kṛtvā sarvvakāmikāmi.āhārapānabhojanaṃ pratilabhate / anābhogaṃ sarvvaṃ saṃpadyate
7.	dvayapārśva limped yathācintita prārthaya(nā)ni vastrāṇi pratilabhate / pṛṣṭhivaṃśe-m ālepayaṃ yathā manasi varttamānaṃ śayyāsanaṃ pratilabhate / karṇṇe lipya marme marmasthāne limpya yathābhiprāyaṃ ā(a)laṃkāravibhūṣaṇaṃ pratilabhate kuṭisthāna limpet aśvayānaṃ bhala(labha)te / dine dine anālāpataḥ pratyuṣe ekaikaṃ rocanagulikā amogharājahṛdayena japatā bhakṣayaṃ pratha-
38b (P.68b7, D.71a2)
i.	[ma]divase ślokasahasraṃ paṭhati dhārayati dine dine dviguṇavarddhamānaṃ paṭhati dhārayati / saptarātre mahāśrutiḥ pravarddhate / dvisaptāhayuktena kinnarīsaha(ma)śvaraghoṣo bhavati / tṛsaptāhaprayuktena mahāsrutiśāgarasannicayo bhaviṣyati / kalaviṅkarutaravitasnigdhamadhuragambhīra(sva)ro bhaviṣyatīti / putrārthī ghṛtena sahta dātavyaṃ kākabandhyastrīṇāṃ api putralābho bhaviṣyanti / ga(ba)ndhanāgārena haḍinigaḍā-
2.	rgaḍasaṃkalāṃ lipeyet saha liptamātre khaṇḍakhaṇḍaṃ sphuṭanti vinaśyanti / aṣṭamī vā caturddaśī vā pañcadaśī vā rocanaguḍikāyā snapaye gandhodake saha saptajaptayā amogharājahṛdayena svayaṃ krodharajenābhiṣiñcayaṃ / saha snapitamātreṇa sarvvapāpā sarvvapūrvvāvaraṇāni vinasyanti / trirātrasnapite nayamantramudra(drā)sādhanasiddhi pratilabhanti / sarvvapratyarthikapratyā(tya)mitrā duṣṭakilbiṣā na prabhavanti / ka-
3.	dā cana<ḥ> sarvvatra sarvvabhayaparimukto bhaviṣyati / na ca bhūyaḥ kadācit pāpamalena lipyate / rocanaguḍikāyā taile nighṛṣya mukhamaṇḍale parimā(r)jya rājānam abhimukhaṃ sthitvā putravat pariṣvajati / mahāprīti labhate sarvvabhayarājakrodhaṃ praśamayanti / śarīre sakalaṃ limpya vadhyārhām api śastraṃ śarīre na kramiṣyati / khaṇḍaṃ khaṇḍaṃ sphuṭiṣyati / śarīre na kramiṣyati
4.	cāpaṃ bhajyati / gorocanaguḍikā kaṇṭhe bandhayan aṭavīkāntāraparvvatagahanadurge praviśet sarvvacoradhūrttataskarayakṣarākṣasamu(ma)nuṣyāmanuṣyacaṇḍamṛgā āśīviṣa.ayagarabhayā na bhaviṣyanti / antarddhitāni bhaviṣyanti / dakṣiṇabāhau bandhayet sarvvatrāparājito bhavati sarvvatra mahāpūjanīyaś ca bhaviṣyati / sarvvatra apratihatamahābalavīryaparākramo
5.	bhaviṣyati / vāmabāhū bandhayet sarvvastrīpuruṣadārakadārika(kā)vallabhatāṃ bhaviṣyati / sarvve darśanakā[ṅ]kṣiṇāṃ bhavanti / mahāpūjārho bhaviṣyati / mahati mahārthāgamo bhaviṣyati / dakṣiṇaṃ jaṃghe bandhayet yojanasahasraṃ gacchati aviśrāntaḥ / gorocanaguḍikā mukhato gṛhya sarṣapaṃ dakṣiṇapāṇinā amoghāṃkuśākarṣaṇaṃ smārya anālāpataḥ / tac ca sthā-
6.	ne abhiruhitvā yatra diśi cittaṃ bhavati strīpuruṣadārakadārikām vā tatra diśi sarṣapam okiret sadya ākarṣito bhaviṣyati / āgamiṣyati / yadi joya(yoja)naśatasthito bhavati tad avaśyāṃ trirātreṇa āgamiṣyati na śaṃsayaḥ / dakṣiṇakare vāmakare gorocanaguḍikā bandhayaṃ yatra spṛśati dhanadhānyahiraṇyamaṇimuktikaśaṃkhaśilāpravāḍarajatajātarūpāṇi vastravibhūṣaṇaṃ ta-
7.	t sarvvākarṣitasañcārito bhavati yatrecchati tatra saṃcariṣyati / bhaktiśālāṃ praviśya bhaktiśāladvāre capaṭe(peṭa)n dātavyaṃ sarvve te a(ra)ndhanapaya(ca)nam āhāram ā<r>karṣitāni bhaviṣyanti / yatrecchati tatra saṃkrāmati gāndhikāvārī gaccha sarvvāvārikapariṣkārā saṃkrāmayati / madhulepanadvāre capeṭan dātavyaḥ sarvvamadyakalaśāt saṃkrāmayati / koṣṭhāgāra capaṭe(peṭa)n dātavyaḥ sarvvadhānyā karṣayati /
39a (P69b7, D.72a3)
1.	rasaśālāyāṃ pravisya cakraṃ spṛśya sarvaguḍarasasarkarakhaṇḍaṃ sa[ṃ]krāmayati / yatrecchati tatra preṣati / naga[ra]dvāre capaṭe(peṭa)n dadyāt saṃdhyākāle sarvvanagaravāstavyā osvāpanakṛtā bhaviṣyanti / śatrū(tru)dvāre nikhanet uccāṭattaṃ kṛtaṃ bhavati / śastrāgāragṛhe kṣipet sarvvāyudhaśastrāṇi stambhitabhañjitāni bhaviṣyanti / rājadvāre nikhanet sarvvapratyarthikapratyamitrāṇi duṣṭasatvā baddhā bhaviṣyanti stambhita-

3.	trecchati tatra saṃkrāmayati / mahājanamadhyām āpūrayan sarvve nagnā bhaviṣyanti sarvvacailotkarṣaṇaṃ bhaviṣyati / mṛdaṅgaṃ lepayet / uccaśare vādayaṃ sarvvaśītavātāśiti(ni) stambhayati vinaśyanti na prabhaviṣyanti / samantayojanaśatāni aśiniṃ nivārayati / samantanagararāṣṭram vādayaṃ sarvvavyādhi.ītyupadravopasargā sāmyanti / sarvvakalikalahavigraha-

39b (P70b8, D.73a4)

40a (P71b8, D.74a5)
1.	rājakalpasiddhir anuttaram / paṭhitasiddhāni sarvvakāryāṇi kariṣyati na saṃśayaṃ padmapāṇi varado nityaṃ bhavate sarvvadehinās pariṣyati yo nityaṃ hṛdayaṃ lokagurū tathā / buddhakoṭisahasrāṇi yathā gaṅgāya vālikāḥ saṃkhyāśatasahasrāṇi puṇyarāśi pravarddhate / cinoti kuśalamūlāni buddhakṛtyaṃ kariṣyati / upavāśenaikajāpena kuryān narapaṇḍitaḥ / bhikṣur vvā bhikṣuṇī vā upāsa-
2.	kam upāśikas tathā<ḥ> sukhāvatīṃ vrajate śīghraṃ mukta kleśaisu(s tu) dāruṇa<ṃ>duḥkhavedanaiḥ / darśanaṃ labhate nityaṃ buddhabodhisatve na saṃśayād iti // atha jāpavidhiṃ vakṣye amoghapāśahṛdayaṃ ja(ya)thāvidhiḥ / jāpakarmāṇi kurvvīta vidyādharatatvasiddhayaḥ / amoghapāśaś ca tiṣṭhabimbasyāgrata gomayamaṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā catu[ra]sraṃ samantataḥ / saṃkhacūrṇṇa<ṃ>suvarṇṇena sūtraye caturasrakam
3.	caturdvāraṃ catuṣkoṇa madhyavibhaktam caturasrakaṃ śvetacandanena citrayet maṇḍalam uttamaṃ padma<ṃ>śatapatreṣu kuṃkumenārccayeta madhyakarkkaṭikāṃ padmaṃ kalasaṃ gandhodapūrṇṇaṃ sthāpayaṃ sarṣapasamanvitam / agarudhūpaṃ ca dahatā pūrṇṇakumbhāni sthāpayam nānāpuṣparacitaṃ kṛtvā arghapādya(tra)ṃ catura sthāpya vidyādhare śucir bhūtvā śucivastrāṇi dhārakaḥ poṣadha<ṃ>-
4.	ṅ ca dhārayaṃ triśuklabhojanaṃ bhoktavyaṃ tṛsnāyi ca bhavitavyaṃ / tṛsa[ṃ]dhyaṃ jāpa kurvvīta amoghapāśahṛdayaṃ aṣṭasatikaṃ trisandhyaṃ āryāvalokitesvara abhimukhaṃ paryaṅkena niṣīdatavyaṃ / jāpamudrāṃ grahetavyaṃ akṣasūtrañ ca dhārayaṃ / tarjjanījyeṣṭhāṃgli prasāraya sāntadāntamana bhūtvā maitracittaḥ samāhitaḥ / dhyānārūḍhasamakāyaṃ saṃsthāpya śāntaprekṣī
5.	anālepataḥ / jāpaṃ dātavyaḥ sahajāpamātrasya mahādharaṇikampo bhaviṣyati / amoghapāśamudrāṃ grahetavyaṃ namaskārañ ca kārayaṃ mahābhūmikampo bhaviṣyati / na bhetavya dṛḍhacittena bhavitavyaṃ sarvvasatvadayāparaḥ / āryāvalokiteśvara.amoghapāśapāde dṛṣṭiṃ sthāpayaṃ yāvat krodharājā saptavārā usmārayet / saptavārā usmāritamātreṇa mahākampa-
6.	nirnnādo bhavati / āryāvalokiteśvarasya lalāṭe raśmayo niścariṣyanti / samantena 'vabhāsayaṃti tañ ca vidyādharaṃ mahatāprītihṛdayaṃ bhaviṣyati / prahasitavadana saṃpraharṣitaromakūpas tato bhagavanta mukham ullokayitavyaṃ saha-m ullokanamātrayā bhagavann āryāvalokiteśvarasya dakṣiṇāmukha sādhukāraśabdo niścariṣyati / akṣiṃ cālayanti samanantara sādhukāraśabdo niścaramāṇasya vi-
7.	dyādharasya sarvvāvaraṇaviśuddhiṃ pratilabhate / mahatā kāyapariśuddhisamanvāgato bhaviṣyati / tathāgatakoṭiniyutaśataśatasahasrāvaropitakuśalamūlā bhaviṣyati / sarvvabodhisatvāvalokanīyo bhaviṣyati / mṛdusukumārakomalaśarīro bhaviṣyati / ākuñcitakuṇḍalakeśasnigdhamadhuravacanapralāpī bhavati / cakṣupariśuddhiñ ca pratilabhate / kāyapariśuddhiñ ca manopariśuddhiñ ca
40b (P72b8, D.75a4)
l.	valipalitavarjito bhaviṣyati / sarvvasatvāvalokitaśuddhir nnāma samādhiṃ pratilabhate / yāvat pratyuṣa āryāvalokiteśvarasvarūpeṇa darśattan dadāti / samāśvāsayati / dakṣiṇapāṇi mū[r]ddhānaṃ sthāpayati / sarvvavarān dadāti / ākāśagamanam vā antarddhānam vā divyacakṣupratilābham vā buddhakṣetrasanda[rśa]nam vā samādhiṃpratilābham vā dhāraṇīpratilābham vā / amoghapāśahṛdayakalpasiddhim vā / śāstrāvāda-
2.	m vā / mantravādam vā / śrū(śru)tadharatvam vā / nidhivādam vā / dhāturvvādam vā / indrajālam vā / devalokagamanam vā / vilapraveśaṃ<m> vā / nāgabhavanapraveśam vā / kṣetravādam vā / yakṣākarṣaṇam vā / rākṣasākarṣaṇam vā / vetāḍotthāpanam vā / vanapraveśam vā / giripraveśanam vā / nagarapraveśam vā / auṣadhapraveśasandarśanam vā / khaḍgavidyādharatvam vā / siddhavidyādharatvam vā / cakrava[r]ttitvam vā / rājalābham vā /
3.	viṣayalābham vā / viṣayapatitvam vā / mahābalapramardanaṃ<m> vā / sañcāraṇam vā / samudragāhanam vā / tat sarvvan dadāti / sarvvakāryasiddhiṃ dadāti / yathā yatrakāmatā paripālayati / pañcānantaryāṇi viśodhayati / satatajāpena / sarvvasatvāvalokanavigatāvaraṇaviśuddhir nnāma samādhi pratilabhate // añjanaṃ sādhayitukāmena tena śuklāṣṭamyāṃ manaḥśilā-
4.	ñjanaṃ tāmrabhājane sthāpya candanamaṇḍalakaṃ karttavyam / ā[r]yāvalokitesvarābhimukhaṃ sthāpya susnātaṃ sucivastrāṇi dhārakaḥ / āryāvalokiteśvaram abhimukhaṃ paryaṅkaniṣaṇṇena japiṭavyaṃ / amogapāśamudrayā sīmābandho bandhayitavyaṃ krodharājā saptavāra usmārayitavyaḥ / triśuklābhuktena bhavitavyaṃ / yasya kulaputro vā kuladuhitā vā / āryāṣṭā-
5.	ṅga-m-upavāsena bhavitavyam / jāpo dātavyaḥ saha japitamātrayā tāmrabhājane jvalati / uṣmāyati phenāyati ca taṃ gṛhya akṣīṇy añjayet / ākāśena riddhyāgacchati / sarvvabuddhakṣetrāṇi paśyati / sarvvatathāgatān dasasu dikṣu buddhān<a> paśyati / mahāvidyādharacakravarttī bhaviṣyati / sarvvavilabhavanāny apāvṛtāni paśyati / sarvvanidhaya utpla(tplu)-
6.	tāni paśyanti / sarvve devabhavabhavanāni apāvṛttadvārāṇi pe(pa)ṣyati yatrecchati tatra gacchati / sarvvayakṣarākṣasabhūtapiśācagrahamanuṣyāmanuṣyaiḥ pratyarthikapratyamitrair adṛśyo bhaviṣyati / aṣṭamyāñ caturdaśyā pañcadaśyām anālāpataḥ / aṣṭasahasrajāpan dātavyam / āryāvalokiteśvarābhimukhaṃ paryaṅkena niṣīditavyaṃ saha jāpamātrayā āryāvalokiteśvaraṃ divye-
7.	nātmabhāvena svarūpeṇāgratam upatiṣṭhati / dakṣiṇapāṇiṃ mūrddhā sthāpayati / vimalodgatāvalokitavikurvveṇa nāma samādhiṃ pratilabhate sarvvatathāgatavyavalokitādhiṣṭhānavyūhasandarśanaprabhāsan nāma ṛddhiṃ pratilabhate / divyaṃ kalpasahasrāṇim āyuḥpramāṇaṃ pratila[bha]te / divyātmabhāvapratilābho bhaviṣyati sarvvatathāgatā paśyati / amitāyu tathāgatam arhantaṃ samyak- saṃbuddhaṃ
41a (P73b8, D.76a4)
1.	saddharmasaṅgāya saṅkariṣyati / sarvvasukhāvatī<vatī>nivāsināñ ca bodhisatvān mahāsatvān pratyupatthāya agratam upatiṣṭhanti divyarūpī bhavati / ākuñcitakuṇḍalakesaś ca bhaviṣyati / padmaprabhāsavimalā nāma kūṭāgāravimāna pratilabhate / ataḥ svaśarīraṃ vajrasāragarbhātmabhāvaṃ pratilabdhukāmena tena vidyādhareṇa taṅ gandhodakakalasapūrṇṇaṃ dvāṣaṣṭisahasravārā parijapya āryāvaloki-
2.	teśvarasya bodhisatvasyāgrataḥ / ahorātroṣitena bhūtvā snapayat saha snapitamātreṇa vajramayaśa[rī]rātmabhāvo bhaviṣyati sarvvatathāgatā cāsya vyavalokayanti / divyaṃ varṣasahasram āyur bhaviṣyati / sarvvayakṣarākṣasābhūtagrahāś ca vasavartinyo bhavati / sarvvadevanāgāś ca pra(prā)durbhaviṣyati / sarvvalaukikāni sādhanakāryāṇi kariṣyati / saha smaraṇamātrayā cittotpādamātrayā sarvva-
3.	grahā sarvvāpasmārā viśati sarvvaṃ kathayanti / satyam asatyam vā bhūtam abhūtam vā / bhavyam abhavyam vā / utpādam anutpādam vā riddhisphītam vā kṣemasubhikṣam vā tat sarvva kathayiṣyanti / evamādīni sarvvakarmmakaraṇīyāni kariṣyaṃti / sarvve karmakarā dāśavat tiṣṭhanti nityasaḥ / athā(tha) pṛthakpṛthak sarvvalokam uttārayitukāmaḥ sarvve vaśagataṃ pratiṣṭhāpayitukāmaḥ
4.	sarvvanarakatiryagyoniyamalokaparimocayitukāmaḥ / amoghajñānavyūhan nāma samādhiṃ pratilabdhukāmaḥ / vimokṣamaṇḍalaṃ sādhayitukāmaḥ / amoghapāśahṛdayakalpa sādhayitukāmaḥ / mahāmoghapāśahṛdayaṃ sādhayitukāmaḥ / tenāya(pi) vidyādhareṇa pṛthakpṛthak pratibimbam āryāvalokiteśvara.amogharūpaṅ karttavyaḥ / jaṭāmakuṭama-
5.	ṇḍitam / eṇeyacarmapaśupativeśadharaṃ sarvvālaṅkāravibhūṣitaṃ paryañkam vā utthitena sudhanadakṣiṇajānuprapatitaṃ vāmataḥ krodharājaṃ mukha.ullokayamānakrodharūpaṣadbhujaṃ / amitābhamakuṭaṃ karttavyaṃ sarvvālaṅkāravibhūṣitaṃ sarvve sadhātukaṃ karttavyam / parvvatārūḍhapadmāsanañ ca varttavyam / śucinā śucivastradhāriṇā tṛsnāyinā ahorā-
6.	troṣitaṃ vidyādhareṇa bhavitavyam pañcagavyopaspṛṣṭena jalasarṣapa-m-abhiṣekābhiṣiktena bhavitavyam / mahākarū(ru)ṇāparigatahṛdayena vyavalokayitavyaṃ yady edṛśena vidhi na karoti sahapaṭhitamātrā sidhyati kiṃ puna jāpakarmmavidhānena / yaś ca vidyādhara aṣṭāśitibhis <tathāgata>koṭigaṅgānadīvālukāsamā tathāgatā 'rhantaḥ samyaksaṃbuddhā pūjayitu-
7.	kāmaṃ draṣṭukāma mahatā kuśalamūlam avāropayitukāmaḥ sarvvasatvān kuśalamūle pratiṣṭhāpayitukāmaḥ sarvvapāpavisuddhikartta(rtu)kāmah tena vidyādhareṇa vā / bhikṣur vvā bhikṣuṇi vā upāśako vā upāsikā vā rājño vā rājñi vā / anyair vvā kaścit sarvva evam vidhayaḥ karttavyaḥ / tena suvarṇṇamayaṃ amoghapaśapratimaṃ āryāvalokiteśvaraṃ karttavyaṃ
41b (P74b7, D.77a3)
1.	yathāvibhavataḥ samvidyamānaṃ / tato prāṅmukhaṃ sthātavya kuṃkuma[ma]ṇḍalakaṃ karttavyaṃ caturasraṃ suvibhayyaṃ(jyaṃ) yaḥ sindūreṇa sūtrayitavya madhyamadhyamaṇḍalake padmamālā karttavyāḥ padmamālāmadhye bhṛṅgāraṇetṛkaṃ karttavyam / nānāpuṣparacitena karttavyaṃ caturbhi gandḥe(ndho)dakapūrnnakumbhā sthāpya / yatha(thā)vibhavapuṣpasamalaṃkṛtaṃ pañcabhi arghapātraiḥ sthāpayitavyaṃ / pañcabhi dhūpakaṭacchai dhūpayitavyaṃ agaru dhūpayatāḥ
2.	ṣoḍaśagandhatailadīpā jvalayitavyam / āryāvalokiteśvara pūjayitavyaṃ puṣpadhūpagandhamālyai ca dātavyaṃ lokeśvarmabhimukhaṃ paryañkena niṣīdayaṃ mudrā<ṃ>m amoghapāśa badhva ātmānam abhiṣiñcayat krodharājā ekavāra usmārayitavyaṃ sarvvatathāgatān adhyālaṃbya manasikāreṇa daśadiśaṃ manasābhivandya namaskarttavyam / jāpamudrā bandhayitavyaṃ jāpo dātavyaḥ / aṣṭaśatajāpena ma-
3.	hādharaṇikampo bhaviṣyati / ātmānaṃ hṛṣyati / jvalati taṃ paśyati / te ca sarvve tathāgatā mukhadarśanam upadarśayaṃti // āryāvalokitesvarasya jaṭāntare jvalati / sādhukāraśabdo niścarati dvitīyam aṣṭottaraśatajāpena mahāsiddhinimitta<ṃ>n darśayati / ā[r]yāvalokiteśvara bāhu prasārayati vidyādharasya mūrddhasire sthāpayati / sarvvatathāgatavyavalokamānan nāma
4.	samādhiṃ pratilabhate / amoghapāsavidyādharo bhaviṣyati / pratyuṣe te ca tathāgatā darśana<ṃ>n dāsyanti / sarvvabuddhakṣetrāṇi darśayanti / sarvvatathāgato kuśalamūlasamanvāgato bhaviṣyati / tṛtīyenāṣṭaśatajāpena āryāvalokiteśvararūpeṇa aṛū(ru)ṇodgatakālasamaye agratam upatiṣṭhati / padmavivalā nāma pāśaṃ dadāti sarvvasiddhi dadāti / ami-
5.	tābhajinaṃ sādhukāran dadāti // yaś ca kaścid ayaṃ āryāvalokiteśvararūpan darśayati / sa catuhṣaṣthi(ṣṭi)tathāgatasahasrāṇi kuśalamūlāvaropito bhaviṣyati / sarvvadevatāś cāsya brahmapramukhā īśvaramaheśvaradevaputrā nityaṃ darśanakāmatā bhavanti / sarvvavyādhivigato bhavati / sarvvavaraparipūrṇṇaś ca nityānubaddhā rakṣāvaraṇaguptaye samvidhāsyanti / sarvva-
6.	pāpavigato bhavati // // atha rūpyamayaṃ / āryāvalokiteśvara.amoghapāśa karttavyā pūrvvavat / rūpaṃ śvetacandanamaṇḍalakaṃ śvetacandanodakagandhapūrṇṇakaṃ sthāpayitavyaṃ pañca arghapātrāṇi sthāpayitavyāni / nānāpuṣpā ukiritavyā śvetacandanadhūpan dahatā / āryāvalokiteśvaraṃ pūjayitavyaṃ dvācatvāriṃśatigandhatailadipā jvālayitavyāḥ / catvāro dhūpaghaṭikā sthāpayitavyaṃ /
7.	krodharājā ekaviṃśa[ti]vārā usmārayitavyaḥ / vidyādhareṇa śucinā śucivastradhāriṇā bhavitavyaṃ / amoghapāśamudrāḥ saptavārāḥ pravarttayitavyā jāpamudrā grahetavyaṃ / paryaṅkaniṣaṇṇena tarjjanyā praśārayitavyaṃ / padmākāraṃ vāmahaste yā aṣṭottaraśatavāraṃ jāpo dātavyaḥ pūrṇṇe aṣṭottaraśate pūrvvasyān diśi śādhukāraśabdo niścarati / ātmānaṃ hṛṣṭaṃ paśyati / sarvvarājāmātyasāntaḥ-
42a (P.76b2, D.78b7)
1. puraparivārā vaśyā bhavanti / nāmā(ma)grahaṇena yojanasahasrād āgacchati / sarvve dāśabhūtā tiṣṭhanti / preṣyākārakā tiṣṭhati / yam ājñāpayasi tat kariṣyanti / sai[va] prabhur bhaviṣyati / mahati pūjyo bhaviṣyati / dvi-r-aṣṭottaraśatajāpena śakrabrahmaviṣṇumaheśvarayamavaruṇakuberādayo vaśyā bhaviṣyanti / a(ā)jñākarās tiṣṭhanti // yam ājñāpayasi tat karoti / catvāraś ca mahārājā sagaṇaparivārā yakṣarākṣasagaṇā va-
2.	śyā bhavanti / tṛ-r-aṣṭottaraśatajāpena candrasūryād ākāśad avataranti / agratam upatiṣṭhanti / rātridevatā ākāśadevata avataranti / agratam upatiṣṭhanti / sarvvakiṃkarakārakās tiṣṭhanti / caturtha(rthā)ṣṭaśatajāpena sarvvanāgā vaśagatās tiṣṭhanti / yāvat sāgaranāgarājā nandopanandaya prabhṛtayaḥ sarvve vaśagatās tiṣṭhanti // tāmramayasyāryāvalokiteśvaraṃ / amoghapāśaṃ raktacanda[na]mayaṃ maṇḍa[la]kaṃ karttavyaṃ //
3.	/ raktapuṣpāvakīrṇṇaṃ karttavyaṃ catvāri dhūpaghaṭikāni dhūpayitavyāni // sa kavi ca dhūpaṃ dvāpañcāśaghṛtadipā jvālayitavyam // arghapātrā pañca sthāpayitavyam / āryāvalokiteśvaraṃ pūjayitavyaṃ / krodharāja ekaviṃśativārā usmārayitavyaṃ / ekaviṃśativārā amoghapāśamudrayā pravarttayitavyaṃ / duṣyapaṭa.agrata paryaṅkena niṣaṇṇena bhavitavyaṃ sarvvasatvālambanena sa-
4.	rvvatathāgata manasikarttavyañ caturddiśaṃ maitrāvalokatena vyavalokayitavyaṃ / sarvvanarakamava(yama)lokāsurapretaviṣayeṣu manasikarttavyām / aṣṭabhir mmahānarakair manasikarttavyaṃ karuṇācittena vyavalokayitavyam / jāpamudrā padmākāra bandhayitavyaṃ japo dātavyaṃ / aṣṭottaraśatapūrṇṇe caturdiśaṃ mahākampaṃ nirnnādo bhaviṣyati / te ca sarvvanarakagata-
5.	yaḥ satvāḥ parimucyante / sarvvanarakadvārāṇi apāvṛtāni paṭhitāni bhasmīkṛtā bhavanti / te ca nārakā satvā sugatigāminā bhaviṣyanti / yāvad bodhiparyavasānaṃ / svayaṃ ca sarvvanārakā duḥkhavedanā parimucyaṃte / divyalakṣaṇālaṃkṛtakāyaś ca bhaviṣyanti / ye ca satvā paśyanti te sarvve avinivarttaniyā bhaviṣyati / dvitīya.aṣṭottaraśatajāpena pātālaga-
6.	tāṃ satvā tamālokavigatānān te amoghapāśahṛdayasyānubhāvena andhakāravigatā bhavanti / raśmayo niścaranti / te satvā parimucyante / āryāvalokiteśvarasya bhavane upapādukār bhaviṣyanti / jarāmaraṇaparivarjitā bhaviṣyanti / yāvad bodhiparyavasānaṃ / samantaprabhāvodgatavimalā nāma samādhi pratilabhate kalpam āyur bhaviṣyati / cyutaḥ sukhāvatyāṃ lokadhātum upapa-
7.	dyate / tasyaiva pādamūle yāvad bodhiparyavasānāṃ tiṣṭhati / sarvvaśokavigataś ca<r> bhaviṣyati ti // // śvetacandanakāstūrikakarpūraṃ anyāni vivi[dha]gandhajātāni / śūkṣmacūrṇṇāni kṛtvā punaḥ supiṣṭaṃ karttavyaṃ kundurukasarjarasaga[ndha]rasenodakaṃ sahabhāvya sitagahanayutaṃ supiṣṭaṃ karttavyaṃ / aṣṭāṅgulapramāṇaṃ āryāvalokitesvara.amoghapāśaṃ karttavyaṃ sarvvālaṃkāravibhūṣitaṃ
42b (P77b1, D.79b6)
1.	jaṭāmakuṭamaṇḍitam eṇeyacarmmapaśupativeśadharaṃ caturbhujam ekapadmadharaṃ / dvitīya pāśadharaṃ tṛtīyaṃ śūladharaṃ caturtham āśvāsanaṃ padmāsanaṃ vyāmaprabhasamalaṃkṛtaṃ karttavya dakṣiṇato āryatārāṃ vāmata vimalamati kāryāḥ / lokeśvarasyādha krodharāja ūrddhvato abhidhābhajinaṃ / tato vidyādhareṇa śucir bhūtvā śucivastrāṇi dhārakaḥ / tṛsandhyaṃ snāyī tṛcailyavasanadhāriṇā tṛsandhyaṃ japo dā-
2 tavyaḥ / aṣṭottaraśataṃ / aṣṭamyā yāvat pūrṇṇapañcadaśyāṃ / nānāgandhair maṇḍalakam upaleptavyam / pa[ñ]caraṅgena rekhitavyaṃ / madhyamaṇḍalake amoghapāśavidyādevatā citrayitavyaṃ jānuprapatatita ūrdhvaprekṣi caturṣu koṇeṣu īśvaramaheśvarayamavaruṇa citrayitavyaṃ / catur<a>dvāravibhaktayaṃ karttavyam // caturṣu koṇeṣu gandhapūrṇṇakā sthāpayatā caturṣu dvāreṣu gandhibalikalasapūrṇṇaṃ sthāpayatā tṛśu[kra]-
3.	bali aṣṭau arghapātrāṇi sthāpayatā nānāpuṣparacitāni karttavyāni agarucandana dhūpayatā aṣṭāvi[ṃ]śatyotaraśatagandhatailadīpam ujvālayitavya patākāsamalaṃkṛtaṃ karttavyaṃ / āryāvalokiteśvaraṃ bodhisatvaṃ pūjā karttavyā pūrvvābhimukha maṇḍalake sthātavyaṃ paṭasūtrapañcaraṅgiśūtraṃ ṣoḍaśahastaṃ dīrgheṇa ekapārśve suvarṇṇakuṇḍalikā bandhayitavyaṃ / mukti-
4.	kuṇḍalikā kuṇḍali bandhayitavyam / sarvva(rpa)vadveṣṭapariveṣṭitaṃ karttavyaṃ / kuṇḍalī kṛtvā madhyamaṇḍalake sthāpya śucinā śucivastre cchādayitavyaṃ / vidyādhare maṇḍalakam abhimukhaparyaṅkaniṣaṇṇena amoghapāśamudrā dvātri[ṃ]sataṃ vārā pravarttayitavyaṃ / kṛtātmarakṣā / maṇḍalabandhaṃ diśābandhaṃ ca karttavyaṃ / krodharājā aṣṭottaravārāśataṃ japitavyaṃ / tato vidyādhareṇa pra-
5.	hasitavadane sphuṭamāna bhavitavyam jāpamudrā grahetavyaḥ / aṣṭottarasahasraṃ japo dātavyaḥ / ekaikaṃ sarṣapaphalakaṃ parijapya tatra paṭasūtram upari kṣeptavyam anālāpataḥ karttavyaṃ pūrṇṇa.aṣṭasahasrataṃ paṭṭasūtrarajjuka jvaliṣyati / nānāraśmayo niśca[ri]ṣyati / sādhukāraśabda niścariṣyati / sādhu sādhu vidyādhara siddha tvayā amoghapāśa gṛhāṇa sa-
6.	rvvakāryāṇi kuruṣva eṣa tvayā mahācintāmaṇipāśaṃ paramasiddhaḥ laukikalokottarāṇi kāryāṇi sādhayiṣyati / tañ cāryāvalokiteśvaraṃ jvaliṣyati / lokeśvaraśarīrā mahāgandhameghā niścariṣyati / ṣaṭyojanapramāṇaṃ gandhena sphariṣyati / yasya gandhaghrātamātrasya sarvvavyādhivigato bhaviṣyati / sarvvaduḥkhāvaraṇakāṣo(kho)rdaviṣacūrṇṇayogād vinasyanti / sarvvasatvā-
7.	nuvarttanīyā bhaviṣyanti // tato vidyādhareṇa taṃ cintāmaṇipāśaṃ grahāya tāmrasamudgake sthāpya / āryāvalokiteśvarasya pādamūle sthāpayitavyaṃ / yadā kāryam bhavati / tadā susnātena śucinā bhavitavya grahetavyaḥ kāryaṃ karttavyaṃ dine dine pūjayitavyaṃ tṛḥkālaṃ / sa ca vidyādhara ākāśagāmī bhavati / pariśuddhātmabhāvapūrvvavidyādharāṇāṃ saha krīḍanti / sarvve vaśagatā
43a (P78a8, D.80b6)
1.	bhaviṣyanti / varṣasahasram āyur bhaviṣyati / cyutaḥ sukhāvatyāṃ lokadhātau saha<r> bhāvyatāyā upapāduka padmakarkaṭikeṣūpapadyate / sarvvavidyādharacakravarttī bhaviṣyati / yādva(vad) bodhimaṇḍalaparyavasānaṃ kalpaśatasahasram āyur bhaviṣyati / tañ cā[r]yāvalokiteśvaraṃ ye dṛṣṭamātrayā pañcānantaryaprabhṛtīni kṣapayiṣyati / sumanaṃ aṣṭottaraśatavārā parijapya āryāvalokiteśvarasya ekaika ni-
2.	vedayitavyaṃ pūrṇṇe aṣṭottaraśata sarvvarājya(jyā)māśā(tyā)ntaḥpuna(ra)parivārā vaśyā bhavanti / ātmanena sādhanaṃ sahiraṇyaṃ sadhānyakaṃ sadāsadāsi karmakarā bhaviṣyanti / tañ ca sumananirmālya gṛhyaṃ gandhapṛyaṅgusahitaṃ satagarakuṣṭhasadāḥ kuṅkumaṃ mā[ṃ]sī sāmakaṃ spṛkaṃ naladaṃ bolaṃ sadāḥ sa[r]ṣapam etat sūkṣmam idaṃ cūrṇṇaguḍikā divyodakaṃ bhāvanayaṃ argadayaṃ pravarañ ca padmavikhyātaḥ kī[r]ttiśriyaṃ //
3.	ādau sarvvavyādhiharaṃ jvarair ū(u)paharaṃ lūtāgaṇḍavisarpiṇaṃ savicarccikaṃ kuṣṭhalohaliṅgas tathā sarvve tāṃ lepanā pravadanti sukhitāṃ <lepanā pravadanti sukhitā> naṣṭā ca te sarvvavyādheṣu / snātvā pāpaharaṃ pravadanti / munayoḥ / āyuḥpradāmaṅgalaṃ rājānaṃ tilakaṃ kṛtvā saṃgrāmagamanaṃ sarvve jaya nityasaḥ strīṇāṃ prahlādakaraṃ vaśīkaraṇam uttamam // kākhordavināsa-
4.	na viṣagaraṃ cūrṇṇa mantrayogas tathā tilakalepanabhojanaṃ snānaṃ sadā nityaśaḥ sarvve te sukham uttaranti jagadā lokeśvaram abravīt / śiraktiśūlaṃ lepanaṃ / akṣiśūla<ṃ>m añjana dantaśūle mukhādhāraṇaṃ kukṣiśūle uṣṇodakaṃ pārśvaśūle ghṛtalepanaṃ / aṅgapratyaṅgaśūle samālabhanaṃ / vaśīkaraṇe pāṇitale lepanam / mahato vyādhe unmardanavisnapanam / yakṣagraha-
5.	vināśanena hastakarmadhūpanaṃ sarvvagrahair vvimucyate / mudritānā mūrddhi śire tilakaṃ hṛda[ya]roge uṣṇodakalavaṇamiśraṃ grahāsāntikaraṇaṃ lājasahitaṃ sṛtyāktā<ṃr> mahāśāntir bhaviṣyati / sarvvatra ekaviṃśativārā parijapya sarvvakalikalahavigrahavivādeṣu gomayamaṇḍalakaṃ karttavyaṃ puṣpāvakīrṇṇa catvāri gandhacūrṇṇā sthāpayatvā<ḥ> / catvāri kṣīrapūrṇṇa-
6.	kāś catvāri arghamā(pā)trāṇi catvāri dhūpakaṭacchūkā agaruturuṣka dhūpayatāḥ / guggulum ekaviṃśativārā parijapya dhūpayatāḥ / āryāvalokiteśvaraṅ gandhamayaṃ pūjayitavyaṃ krodharājā aṣṭottaravārāśataṃ japya sarṣapaṃ parijapya caturddiśaṃ kṣipitavyaṃ / amoghapāśahṛdayaṃ / ekanavativārām usmārayitavyam / sarvvakalikalahavigrahavivādeṣūttaravādī bhaviṣyati / sa-

43b (P79a7, D.81b6)
1.	prajvālya pūrṇṇacaturdaśyāṃ karttavyaṃ paripūrṇṇo jāpavidhiḥ śakro devānām indrasabalavāhanam avatariṣyati / agratam upatiṣṭhanti / ājñākarā bhaviṣyanti / sarvve vaśagatā tiṣṭhanti / yaṃ mārgayasi tam dadāti sthāpya śakratvasthāne eta citta na kṣamate / brahmāṇaṃ pādam avaṣṭayya(bhya) japet / aṣṭottaraśataṃ pūrṇṇe aṣṭottaraśate brāhmaṇaṃ purataḥ svarūpeṇa-m upatiṣṭhati / sarvvavarāṇi pravārayati / yathākāmaṅgamā
2.	manasi varttate / nārāyaṇaṃ guggulu navativārā parijapya dhūpaṃ jalasarṣapeṇa ekaviṃśatijaptena pūrvvo dātavyaṃ nārāyaṇa svarūpeṇa agratam upatiṣṭhati / sarvvakarmakaro bhava<bhava>ti / yāvad viśvarūpan dadāti / hiraṇyakasi pā(vā) yāmam anuprayacchati / mahānārāyaṇabalavego bhaviṣyati / maheśvaraṃ mukham avalokya dakṣiṇakarṇṇe aṣṭottaram vāra śataṃ parijapya sarṣa-
3.	pam ekaviṃśativārā parijapya mūrddhato dadyād gugguludhūpa dātavyaḥ saptavārā parijapya arghapādyaṃ sa nivedayitavyaṃ / tato maheśvarapratimā calati / sādhukāraśabdo niścarati / tato maheśvararūpeṇa agratam upatiṣṭhati / sagaṇaparivārā sarvve vaśagatā tiṣṭhanti / sarvvakāmāni varaṃ prayacchati / mahākālam āvāhayitukāmena guggulum aṣṭotta-
4.	raśatavāraṃ parijapya rudhiramadya nivedayi[ta]vyam / ekaviṃśativārā sarṣapam parijapya mahākālam upari kṣeptavyam / āvāhito bhaviṣyati / preṣyakarā bhaviṣyati sarvvakarmmakaro bhaviṣyati / yatra preṣayati tatra gacchati / sagaṇaparivārā bhaviṣyanti / sarvvayakṣabhavanāni apāvṛtadvāraṇi bhaviṣyanti / yam icchati tat karoti / sarvvatṛṇasāmanya bhavi-
