6.	gena vimānāni-m upanāmayanti / śucinā śucivastraprāvṛtena susnāta satyavādina maitracittaṃ samāsthāpya sarvvasatvadayā sadā / mahākaruṇān adhyālambya jāpaṃ kurvvato nityasaḥ // // iti jāpavidhipaṭalam // // athāto homavidhim vakṣye sarvvakarmakaraṃ subhaṃ śāntipauṣṭikañ cābhicārakaṃ rakṣāḥ sarvvavyādhiharaṃ kākhordanāsanaṃ vaśīkara-
7.	ṇaṃ sadā / gṛharakṣā karttavyā kāmena padmaṃ kaṭukatailāktāṃ ārkkakāṣṭhāsama(mi)dbhir agni prajvālya aṣṭottaraśataṃ juhūyāt / aṣṭottaravāraśata<ṃ>m amoghapāśahṛdayaṃ pravarttayitavyaṃ sapta- // // vāra krodharājā pravarttayi- // // tavyaṃ / mahāgṛharakṣa bhavati / sarvvakalikalahavigrahavivādeṣu sarvvapratyarthikapratyā(tya)mitreṣu sarvvavyādhiṣu sarvvajvareṣu sarvva.ī-
44a (P80a4, D.82b4)
1.	tyupadravopasargā / sarvvagrahā sarvvadu[r]bhikṣakāntāra- marakamahamarastrīpuruṣadārakadārikāmareṣu praśamiṣyanti / sarvve devatā cāsya rakṣāvaraṇaguptiṃ samvidhāsyanti / īśvaramaheśvaraś ca nityānubaddhā bhaviṣyanti / āryāvaloki[te]śvaraś ca satatasamitaṃ darśanakāmatā bhaviṣyanti / ghṛtāhutisarṣapaguggulena / aṣṭottarasahasrahomena / sakalaviṣayarāṣṭrāna- / gareṣu grāmakarvvaṭajanapadeṣu mahāśānti
2.	bhaviṣyanti / āryāvalokiteśvarasyāgrataḥ pūrṇṇamāsyā śukla<kha>puṣpāṇā gandhapriyaṅgutagaraṃ kaṭukataila(lā)ktānām aṣṭottarasahasraṃ juhūyāt / rājāmātyaśāntaḥpuraparivārā grāmanagaranigamajanapadahastyaśvagomahiṣyā vaśyā bhaviṣyanti / apāmārgasamidhānā dadhimadhughṛtāktānāṃ śuklapuṣpāṇā<ṃ>m aṣṭottarasahasraṃ juhūyāt / grāmasahasraṃ labhate / āryāvalokiteśvara-
3.	syāgrataḥ / dadhimadhughṛtāktānāṃ padmadaṇḍasamidhānām aṣṭottraśataṃ juhūyāt / rātryāntraye(tryantare)na dīnārāṣṭra(ṣta)śataṃ labhate / kumudānā padmadaṇḍamūlapatra aṣṭottarasahasra juhūyāt sarṣapāktāṃ khadirakāṣṭai(ṣṭhai)r agni prajvālya sarvvavināyakebhyo parimucyate // śuklāṣṭamyāṃ [u]poṣya śuklā vastrāṇi prāca(va)ret / śucinā susnātena bhavitavyam / candanaṃ pṛyaṅgukā-
4.	ṣṭhaṃ jalaṃ ghṛtasarṣapāktā juhūyād aṣṭottarasahasraṃ arddharātrīsamayena yakṣiṇīyād āgacchati // pañcasahasrāṇi rūpakānā prayacchati / kṛṣṇacaturddaśyām ahorātroṣitena samudragāmini nadīkūle gatvā bilvakāṣṭhair agniṃ prajvālya bilvapuṣpāṇāṃ ghṛtā madhvāktānām aṣṭasahasraṃ juhuyāt tato yakṣakanyād āgacchati sarvvālaṅkāravibhūṣitā / arddharātīd āgacchati
5.	ratnapa(pe)ṭaka<ṃ>n dhārayati tena vidyādhareṇa grahetavyan na ca mukhena nirīkṣitavyam / punar aṣṭaśataṃ japitavyaṃ bhājanam utsṛjya pādayo patati / vidyādharam ariṣyāmi / tato vidyādhareṇa krodharājā ekaviṃśativārā pariya(ja)pya tato yaṃ kāryaṃ bhavati / tadārddharātre aṣṭaśatavārāt jāpo dātavyaḥ / yakṣiṇyād āgacchati sarvvakāryāṇi kariṣyati preṣyā
6.	bhavati / sarvvakarmmakārikā bhaviṣyanti / kṛṣṇacaturddaśyā yatra nidhiṃ bhavati / tatra gatvā aṣṭottarasahasra japed balividhānañ ca sthātavyaṃ / ārya.amogharājā pūjā karttavyāḥ sumanapuṣpaphalaṃ pattrañ ca sarṣapaṃ ghṛtāktām aṣṭottarasahasrāhutiṃ kṛtvā sarṣapodakam ekaviṃśativārā parijapya dharaṇi tāḍayitavyan tataḥ sarvvanidhānāni uttiṣṭhanti gṛhītvā ratnatraye bhāgan dātavyaṃ śeṣa svayaṃ grahe-
7.	tavyaḥ / vibḥītakakāṣṭhair agni prajvālya tilataṇḍulānā ghṛtāktānām aṣṭottarasahasraṃ juhūyād rājā śāntaḥpuraparivārā śānta(sa)caturaṅgabalakāyaṃ sadhanaṃ sadhānyaṃ sahiraṇyaṃ suvarṇṇaṃ vaśyā bhavanti / yāvajjīvena tiṣṭhanti / gaurasarṣapaṃ campakapuṣpaṃ padmasahitaṃ ghṛtāktānā<ṃ>m aṣṭottarasahasra juhūyāt sarvvaviṣaya vāstavyā strīpuruṣadārakadārikā vaśyā bhavanti // śālītuṣasa-
44b (P81a5, D.83b4)
1.	lavaṇam ekanavativārāj juhūyāc cāturvvala(rṇa)sya lokasya vaśyā bhavanti // agastikāṣṭhair agni prajvālya madhughṛtāktā<ṃ>nām arkapuṣpāṇām aṣṭottarasahasraṃ juhūyāt / āryāvalokiteśvarasya pūjā karttavyā dine dine dināraśataṃ labhate / palāśaṃ lājā samidhā sahasraṃ juhūyāt suvarṇṇasahasraṃ labhate / tṛratnapūjā karttavyaṃ vibhaktavya punaḥ prādurbhavati / pūrṇṇapañcadaśyām ahorātroṣite samudragā-
2.	minyā nandyāṃ(dyāṃ) kaṭīmātram avatīrya aṣṭottaraśataṃ japya sarṣapaṃ japya ekaviṃśativārā parijapya tatra nadyo krodhana tāḍayitavya tato udakamadhyāṃ kanyā nnirgacchati / prabhūtaprabhūtā(ratnā)ni dadāti / gṛhītvā arddhabhāgaṃ ratnatraye pūjayitavya āryāvalokiteśvarapūjā karttavyā / pūrṇṇapañcadaśyā<ṃ>m ahorātroṣitena bhagavato āryāvalokiteśvarapūjāṃ kṛtvā apāmārgasamidhānā madhughṛtā sarjara-
3.	sāktānām aṣṭottaravārāśata parijapya sahasravārā juhūyāt / ātmānā nāma grahetavyaṃ / sarvvapāpāvaraṇāni vimucyate / sarvvavyādhayaḥ sarvvarogād vimucyate / sarvvapratyarthikapratyamitrāṇi vinaśyanta // arkkakāṣṭhaṃ tilasarṣapaṃ gugguluṃ ghṛtāktāṃ juhūyāt mahāśāntir bhaviṣyati / lājākṣīrāktā ghṛtasarṣapaṃ śvetacandana juhūyād aṣṭaśataṃ pauṣṭike pa-
4.	ramaṃ śubham / caturddaśyāṃ karavīrapuṣpañ ca saptalavaṇamiśritaṃ kaṇṭakakāṣṭhasamidhāṃ kaṭu[ka]tailāktābhicārukam / padmaṃ candanaṃ sarṣapaṃ ghṛtāktā ekaviṃśati japtā aṣṭottaraśataṃ juhūyāt krodhamantra saptavārā usmārayet / anāvṛṣṭiṃ mahāvarṣadhara patati / yāvadājñāyā pravarṣati / tañ ca bhaśmanā gṛhya ekaviṃśativārā parijapya ākāśe kṣipid ativṛ-
5.	ṣṭiṃ praśamayati / caturdiśaṃ kṣipet mahāsīmābandho bhaviṣyati / megham abhimukhaṃ kṣipet sarvvavātā(ta)meghāsani na prabhaviṣyanti / sainyastambhana<ṃ>n tilataṇḍulahomena / paracakranidhāpanaṃ saptabījakahomena bilasādhanaṃ pataṅgahomena // arkkapattraṃ ghṛtānāṃ juhūyāt sarvvaviladvārāṇi apāvṛtāni bhavanti / vanapraveśe kumudaṃ tagaraṃ sarṣapaṃ ghṛtāktānāṃ sarvvavana-
6.	vivarāṃ / apāvṛtāni bhavanti / sarvvadravyauṣadhaya svarūpeṇa tiṣṭhanti / palāsakāṣṭhair agniṃ prajvalya maricam aṣṭasahasraṃ juhūyāt śakrabrahmaviṣṇumaheśvarādīni [a]vatarati / agratam upatiṣṭhanti / suvarṇṇapuṣpaṃ karṇṇikārikapuṣpaṃ cotpalānā ghṛtāktānā<ṃ>m aṣṭasahasraṃ juhūyāc candrasūryam avataranti / sarvvavarān pravārayanti / ākāse <na> gacchanti / rājavṛkṣasamidhānāṃ kuryā śatapuṣpaśa-
7.	tāvarīpataṅgacandanaṃ sarṣapaṃ yavaghṛtāktānām ekaviṃśati ahuti tṛsandhya divasāni sapta mahārājāṃ vaśīkaraṇaṃ bhavati śāntaḥpuraparivārā bhavanti / sarvvabhūtagrahayakṣarākṣasanigrahakaraṃ taṇḍulasarṣapaṃ lavaṇahomena sarvvatra amoghapāśamudrayā pravarttayitavyaṃ / sarvvatra krodhamudrayā mantram etā samanusmaret // ahutividhiḥ // // ato amoghapā-
45a (P82a5, D.84b4)
1.	śahṛdayasya mahākalparājasya paṭavidhānaṃ pravakṣāmi / saha darśanamātrayā avicī(vīci)paryantapañcānantaryakārīti viśuddhyate / dvihastamātraṃ paṭaṃ kuryāt / acchinnadaśagate pāpagate / arbudaṃ suślakṣṇaṃ suśodhitaṃ samantacaturasraka maunīvratena poṣadhikena citrakareṇa śucinā śucivastradhāriṇā bhavitavyam / tṛśuklabhukle(kte)na trisnāpi(yi)nā bhavitavyam / aśleṣakai raṅgair navabhāja-
2.	nasthair navena śucinā kūrccakena / āryāvalokiteśvaraṃ amoghapāśaṃ citrayitavyam / utthitam vā niṣaṇṇam vā paryaṅkaniṣaṇṇam vā likhya jaṭāmakuṭamaṇḍita / eṇeyacarmapaśupativeṣadharaṃ sarvvālaṅkāravibhūṣitaṃ ṣoḍaśabāhu karttavyā saumyavadanaṃ prahasitavada[na]netram amitābhajinamakuṭaṃ dakṣiṇābhimukhaṃ navacandra makuṭāntare / dvayo hastau vajrāñjalyo ūrddhagatau padmākā-
3.	raṃ dve ṣa(ha)stau adhastāt samantamukhamudrayā karttavyāḥ / dve(dvi)haste jāpamudrayā dakṣiṇatarjanī prasārya padmākāraṃ grahetavyaṃ / antarā akṣamālā dhārya dvayo amoghapāśamudrayā karttavyaṃ / ekena pāṇinā tṛśūlaṃ dvitīyena padmaṃ tṛtīyena amoghapāśapustakaṃ / caturthena amogcpāśasvarūpalagnaṃ pañcamenāśvāsaṃ ṣaṣṭhena kuṇḍikaṃ saptamena
4.	nānāratnabhājanapūrṇṇa aṣṭamena nānāpuṣpapuṭaṃ karttavyaṃ vyāmaprabhāla[ṃ]kṛtaṃ / nānāpuṣpavicitropaśobhitaśarīra karttavyam / potalakakuṭilākūṭāgāraṃ karttavyaṃ suvibhaktaṃ caturasraṃ catusthūṇaṃ jagati śobhanaṃ nānāratnavicitritakhacitaṃ padmamālopaśobhinī vajramaṇḍalībhi racitaṃ nānāpuṣpaśākhakadalyopaśobhitaṃ dakṣiṇe-
5.	na ūrddhvah sūryaḥ / vāmata candraḥ śuddhāvāsakāyikair devaputraiḥ karttavyaṃ / nānāpuṣpapaṭṭahastair bhagavanta pūjayamāna parvvatam padmākāraṃ karkkaṭikopaśobhitaṃ sadaṇḍa puṣkiriṇyopaśobhitam mātsā(tsyā)kīrnnaṃ makarahansasārasajīvaṃjīvakacakravākaracitaḥ / nānāmaṇiratnasaṃskṛtaṃ parvvatasyopari padmāsanaṃ karttavyaṃ si[ṃ]hāsanaṃ
6.	parivṛtaṃ dakṣiṇapārśve īśvaraṃ karttavyam / bahi kuṭisthāne vāmataś ca maheśvaraḥ sarvvālaṅkāravibhūṣitam / abhyantarasthāne āryāvalokiteśvarasya dakṣiṇataḥ / āryatārā karttavyā vaśatāṃgi śalajjaṃ vāmata vimalamatiḥ / āśvāsahasta / sa(su)dhanaṃ jānuprapatitaṃ karttavyam / madhyakarkaṭikasthāne krodharājā karttavyā caturbhuje krodharūpa praharaṇa-
7.	hastaṃ pāśakhaḍgas tṛśūlaṃ padmabhujaṃ paryaṅkārddhakṛtaṃ padmāsana ākuñcitaśarīram / ūrddhaśire riṣayo karttavyaṃ / dakṣiṇavāmata stūyamānam / tato 'ddhastā dakṣiṇa<ṃ>vāmayo mahāvajraśikharo mahāvajrapāśahastaḥ kāryah / yamavaruṇakuberavāsavadevagaṇā karttavyāḥ puṣkiriṇyāntare vāmapārśvataḥ śakra brahmañ ca kra(ka)rttavyaḥ / <ś>catvāraś ca mahārājā vā-
45b (P83a3, D.85b2)
l. mapārśvataḥ sannaddhabaddhakavacā karttavyāḥ / tasyādha<stadha>stāt padmakulasurī dūtī vyagrahastāḥ kāryāḥ śriyā sarasvatyā bhīmā rātṛdevatāḥ pṛthivīdevatā sasyadevatā īśvarapārśve bhṛkṭi maheśvarasya pāṇḍaravāsinī dūtyantare ca karttavyāḥ / ekajaṭā rākṣasī caturasrasamante padma(rva)taṃ saptaratnakhacitaṃ karttavyaṃ / nānādrumalatākīrṇṇa karttavyaṃ puṣpasamalaṃkṛtaṃ puṣkaṛa(ri)ṇyopaśo-
2.	bhitaṃ / nānāpuṣparacitaṃ kīrṇṇa karttavyaṃ / siṃhavyāghrākīrṇṇaṃ kumbhāṇḍanāgapotakaṃ vāmato pañcamahāguhyakaṃ karttavyaṃ / adhastāt pañcamahānāgarājā sarvvatra parvvatam āsanaṃ nānāpuṣparatnahastā bhagavantaṃ mukham ullokayamānaṃ karttavyaṃ / āryāvalokiteśvarasya pādamūle vidyādharaṃ jānuprapatitā karttavyāḥ puṣpadhūpakaṭu(ṭacchu)kahastā bhagavantaṃ mukham ullokayamānaṃ kartta-
3.	vyaṃ jagati mūrddhat amitābhayi(ji)naṃ śākyarājaṃ lokendrarājaṃ mahāmuni karttavyaṃ / saha darśanamātreṇa ayaṃ duṣyapaṭaṃ janmaśatasahasraṃ / avīcisañcitaṃ pañcānantaryakārakaṃs te sarvve naśyanti vinaśyanti / sarvvāṃ(ttvāṃ)ś ca tān sarvvān parimucyanti / prāg eva śuddhasatvānāṃ śraddhādhimuktikānāṃ ratnapūjārthikānās te ihaiva janmani mahāphalam mahānuśaṃ<m>sā
4.	pratikāṃkṣitavyā catu[ḥ]ṣaṣṭīnān tathāgatagaṅgānadīvālukakoṭīniyutaśatasahā(ha)srāṇām antike kuśalamūle samanvāgato bhaviṣyati / avaropitakuśalamūlaś ca bhaviṣyati / ekabhāvadarśanam āryāvalokiteśvaram amoghapāśahṛdaya paṭaladuṣyaṃ asya ca darśanamātrayā duṣyapaṭalam āryāvalokiteśvara samanvāharati / darśana<ṃ>-
5.	ñ ca dāśyati / ākrandamātrayā sadyaṃ vyavalokayati sarvvāvaraṇāni pravā(sa)yati / amogharājadhāraṇīhṛdayasiddhā bhavanti / vyādhitasya mahato vyādhiparipīḍitasya nānāvyādhiduḥkhaśatasahasravyākulasya<s> te sarvve saha darśanamātrayā sarvvavyādhayaḥ sarvvaduḥkhā sarvvarogā parimucyante / vigatāvaraṇavyādhayo bhaviṣya-
6.	ti / antaśas tiryagyonigatām api saha darśanamātrayā parimucyante / ayaṃ duṣyapaṭaṃ mahatā pūjāsatkāreṇa mahatā parivāreṇa mahatā tūryatāḍāvacaraiḥ cchattradhvajapatākaiḥ samalaṃkrtvāṃ(taṃ) kṛtvā mahānagare vā rājakule vā vihāre vā janapade vā gṛhe vā grāme vā pravesayet saha praviṣṭamātrayā duṣyapaṭaṃ sarvvakalikalahavigrahavivādaṃ durbhikṣa-
7.	kāntāramara[ka]ḥ svacakraparacakrabhayaṃ pra[śa]miṣyanti / sarvvayakṣarākṣasabhūtapisācapūtanakaṭapūtanaskando 'pasmāracchāyā praśamiṣyanti / kṣemaṃ subhikṣaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ bahujanasaukhyaṃ bahulā bhaviṣyanti / mahatā prītisaumanasyena bhaviṣyanti / ṣaṭpāramitānugamā bhaviṣyanti / cyutāḥ sarvve asya darśanamātrayā sukhavatyā lokadhātāv upapatsya-

1. nte // sarvve ca satvā sukhāvatīnu(ṣu) gāminā bhaviṣyati // saptatikalpaśatasahasrāṇi jātī jātī(ti)smaro bhaviṣyati / padmaprabhāsavimalaviśuddhirājā nāma bodhisatvā mahāsatvānām alabdhasamādhiṃ pratilabhate / padmapratiṣṭhitanāmavidhyādharacakravarttī pratilabheti // // paṭavidhā<nā>navidhikalpam // //
Amoghapāśakalparāja – Part IV
46a1 (P84a3, D.86b3)
1.	athāto amoghapāśahṛdaye mahākalpamaṇḍalaṃ vyākhyāsyāmi / nadī-
2.	tīre padmaśaraśi vā utpalaiḥ saram eva ca puṣpodyāne phalārāma vimānāni śuddhabhūmisadāḥ suśodhitaṃ bhūmibhāgañ ca kṛtvā pagatatṛṇatūlakeśe tuṣabhasmāṅgārasaṃkarakūṭakaṭharṇṇakamveṣu leṣṭaśarkkarākaṭharṇṇa.asti(sthi)kapālakaṃkālakamṛtakuṇapavarjitaṃ bhūmibhāgahradaprasravaṇe-m uttarottarata prasravaṇaḥ samasamā jalaṃ vidyate / suvibhaktaṃ dakṣiṇapaścimasya uttarapūrvvā ca yatra vṛkṣo-
3.	paśobhitaḥ / yac cābhiramya<ṃ>manasavarṇṇatejasamanvitan tasmi sthāna samāsādya japtvā vai jalasarṣapaṃ prakṣipet samantena eṣa bhūmiśodhanam uttamaṃ / ekatriṃśativārā parijapya sarṣapaṃ krodharājena japya kṣeptavyaṃ / caturdiśa diśābandhaṃ maṇḍalabandhañ ca sīmābandhan tathaiva ca samantena yojanaśataṃ bhaviṣyati / apasarpata(ṇa) sarvvaduṣṭāś ca sarvvavighnavināyakaḥ / kṣaṇenāpi na tiṣṭhanti
4.	vrajanti ca diśo daśah / duṣṭāprāṇāduṣṭasatvāś ca duṣṭaś caṇḍamṛgāś ca ye sarvve te praśamaṃ yānti rakṣanti sarvvadevatāḥ tasmi sthānapradeśe na vinaset maṇḍalaṃ viduḥ caturasramaṇḍalaṃ māpyapārśve dvātriṃśahastakaṃ / suvibhaktaṃ catuḥkoṇaṃ samaṃ bhāgasobhitaṃ caturdvāraṃ catustoraṇayojitaṃ / pañcavarṇṇikacūrṇṇena yojan tato maṇḍalam ālikhet pañcaraṅgikasūtreṇa sūtrayitvā śve-
5.	tacandanakīlakaiḥ śuklapakṣe aṣṭamī pañcadaśyāpi śobhane tithinakṣatre maṅgalye cābhilakṣayaṃ lekhayan maṇḍalam uttamaṃ // prathamaṃ śvetarekhena dvitīyam raktakas tathā / tṛtīyaṃ pitakaṃ jñeya caturtham ākāśakas tathā / pañcamam añjanavarṇṇakas madhye garbbhamaṇḍalakaṃ bhaktasuvibhaktavisāradaḥ / navakūrccake navabhājanastha likhya garbhamaṇḍale padmaṃ sahasrapatra likhya
6.	savedikākārasamantataḥ padmamālā samantena raṅgacitrair vvirājitaṃ / tatra madhye sthāpya bhagavān buddhaśākṛ(kya)rājaṃ mahāmuniṃ / amitābhaṃ lokendrarājñas tathāgato dakṣine lokeśvaranāthaḥ / amoghapāśaveṣadharaṃ mantrānusarātaḥ / vāmato vajrapāṇiś ca cāmaravajrahastabhṛkuṭīmukhaṃ jvālāgarbhaṃ āryāvalokiteśvarasyānusandhi tārādevyā vimalaga(ma ?)ti
7. karttavyāḥ / avalokiteśvarasyādhastān mahāgaurīpāṇḍaravāsinīmaheśva(hāśve)tādaṇḍu(ṇda)kamaṇḍaluhastā karttavyāḥ / tasyādhastād ekajaṭārākṣasī kāryāḥ / vikṛ[ta]daṃ<ta>ṣṭrakapālamālādhāriṇyā pratiṣṭhā sarvvayajñopavīti caturbhujā dakṣiṇa- vāmābhyāṃ vajraparaśu vāmāṅgulikāsuravināyakatarjayantī karttavyāḥ / vāmato brahmā(hma)veṣadharaṃ karttavyā āryāvaloki-
46b (P85a1, D.87b1)
1.	teśvaraḥ / jaṭāmakuṭamaṇḍitaṃ / vāmadakṣiṇa īśvaramante(he)śvaraṃ karttavyaṃ / tasyādhastāṭ śriyā devyā sarasvatīrātridevatāsasyadevatāḥ phalapuṣpadevatā karttavyāḥ / tasyādhastāt samantena yamavaruṇakuberādayaḥ karttavyāḥ ṛṣayaś ca sthāpyā jaṭāmakuṭamaṇḍitaḥ koṇe caturo mahārājā sannaddhabaddhakavacā sthāpyā antarā antaramadhye ca kāryāṅkuśa<ṃ>paraśuvajradaṇḍapāśaśaṃka-
2.	lā maṇḍalagarbhasyā bahi brahmā(hma)rudra.umādevī / brahmāṇa pārśve sarasvatī bhṛkuṭirūpiṇī / tasyāntare<ta> daṇḍakamaṇḍaluś ca ekakoṇayo amoghapāśahṛdavimokṣamaṇḍalaṃ dharmaparyāyaṃ / ekasmin śivāvahā ca tato hayagrivaṃ krodha sthāpayitavyaṃ / pūrvvadvāre mahābhairavanetropasthāpayitavyaṃ / dakṣiṇena mahākelikilan nāma vajravināyako vajraparaśupāśahastaḥ kāryaḥ paścimena padmakulasunda-
3.	rī dūtī karttavyaṃ savyagrahastā / uttareṇa mahāvajraśikharo nāma gaṇa vajrapāśahasto kāryaḥ / tato praveśadvāreṇa gandhodakaparipūrṇṇakaṃ kalasasthāpya cīnavastrāṃ subaddha madhyamaṇḍalake yathābhiprāyaṃ kalaśaṃ suvarṇṇacchāditena sthātavyaṃ / candana<ṃ>kuṃkumakāstūrikakarpūrodakena sthāpya sarvvalaṃkāravibhūṣitaṃ karttavyaṃ / cīnāṃ suvastreṇa cchādayitavyaṃ puṣpamālyo-r a-
4.	laṃkarttavyaṃ suvarṇṇakaṭakena-m upari sthātavyaṃ catvāro dhūpaka[ṭa]cchāni sthāpayitavyā<ṃ>ni / agarudhūpayatāḥ pañcaraṅgikapaṭṭasūtrapramāṇaṃ tatra yo yajñopavitapramāṇaṃ karttavyaṃ suvarṇṇamayaṃ tṛśūlaṃ kaṭakaṃ bandhayitavyaṃ dvitīyapārśve padmakuṇḍikā bandhayitavyaṃ muktikāratnakhacitaṃ / maṇḍalikāpariveṣṭukalaśam upari sthāpayitavyaṃ pūjāśatkāraṃ karttavyaṃ / amogapā-
5.	śahṛdayena aṣṭottarasahasravārā parijapitavyaṃ / sarṣapenākṣataḥ karttavyaḥ / candanakarpūrakāstūrikena vāsacūrṇṇena vā / vāsayitavyaṃ caturṣu dvārasthāneṣu gandhapūrṇṇakalaśā tṛśuklabali sthāpayitavyaṃ / dvātriṃśati arghapātrāṇi sthāpayitavyaṃ suvarṇṇabhājanarūpyabhājana sthāpayitavyaṃ / aṣṭau gandhapūrṇṇakā sthāpayitavyāḥ / aṣṭau gandhaśuklapūrṇṇakā
6.	sthāpayitavyāṃ / aṣṭau nānārasagandhapū[rṇa]kā sthāpyaḥ / aṣṭau gandhadadhikṣiragandhapūrṇṇakā sthāpyaḥ / astau-r ū(-r u)pahārāh / catuḥṣaṣṭir upahārādyair bali māmsarudhirapalāṇḍugṛjya(ñja)nakakubhojyakupuṣpavirahitāḥ śucinā śucayaḥ karttavyaḥ / nānāpuṣpāvakīrṇṇañ ca karttavyan nānācchatradhvajapaṭapatāsa(ka)m alaṃkaritavyaṃ / dvātriṃśati dhūpakaṭacchāni sthāpayitavyaṃ / nānādhūpavicitraṃ tāṃ dahatā / aṣṭottaraśa-
7.	ta gandhatailadīpā jvalayitavyaṃ / maṇḍalasya samantena padmamālā citrayitavyaṃ / vidyādhareṇa śucinā śucivastrāṇi dhārayitavyaṃ / tṛsnāyinā bhavitavyaṃ tṛcailavasanadhāriṇā bhavitavyaṃ śucisahāyānuvarttinyā bhavitavyaṃ yāvat praveśavidhikṛtā amoghapāśahṛdayenābhiṣicyābhiṣiktamātraś ca sarvvabuddhaparigṛhīto lokanāthā-r adhiṣṭhitah padmakulapadmadharamahāmaṇḍala.amoghahṛda-
47a (P85b7, D.88b1)
1.	yasiddhiś cābhimukhī bhavanti yan cintayati-s tathaiva sarvvakāryāṇi karoti / sarvvasamayāmoghapraviṣṭaḥ sarvvadharmatāvimokṣamaṇḍalaṃ dharmasamayasiddheti bhavanti / sarvvakarmāvaraṇavinirmuktaḥ / sarvvasukhānugāmi divyamānuṣyakā śriyam anubhūyati / devāsurayakṣarākṣasagaṇānugatabhāgavastrā yoya(ga)maṇḍalam anupreśakāś ca bhavanti tāḍanabhaṇḍanakīlanadahanataṛjanakṣepaṇamāraṇavaśīkaraṇāni na prabhava-
2.	nti / ye ca tasya kurvvanti teṣām eva śarīre śatasahasraguṇitaṃ śirasi nipatanti / na ca pratyaṅgirā nānyasakti mocayituṃ vinā tena vidyādhareṇa mocayituṃ / atha taṃ vidyādharaṃ maṇḍalābhimukhaṃ paṭaṃ sthāpya amoghapāśahṛdayam aṣṭottarasahasraṃ japatā amoghopāśamudrayā-m aṣṭottaravārā śataṃ pravarttayitavyaṃ / krodharājā ekaviṃśativārā pravarttayitavyaṃ / tato maṇḍalakapratimā
3.	raśmayo niścaranti / nānāvarṇṇāni tan ca kalasaṃ jvalati / tañ ca duṣyapaṭakaṃ saṃpūrṇṇavarṇṇakaṃ jvalati / mahākampaś ca bhavati / vidyādharaṃ gṛhyati / duṣyapaṭe sādhukāraśabdo niścare(ra)ti / sādhu sādhu vidyādharaḥ siddha tvayā āryāvalokiteśvarmamoghapāśahṛdayaṃ / ayañ ca te pāśasiddhaṃ / eṣa tvayā cintāmaṇipāśah paramasiddhas tvayā sarvvakarmāṇi kuraṣva
4.	paramasiddhas te maṇḍalavidhivistaras taṃ codakaṃ gṛhya kalasapūrṇṇam aṣṭaśatavārā parijapya puṣyanakṣatre snapitavyaṃ śuklatithimaṅgalye saha snapitamātreṇa sarvvakāyavimalipariśuddhaṃ pratilabhate / ta(pa)ñcātmabhāvapariśuddhiṃ pratilabhate / pañcasrotrapariśuddhiṃ / pañcacakṣupariśuddhiṃ / pañcaghrāṇapariśuddhiṃ / pañcajihvāpariśuddhiṃ / pañcavijñānapari-
5.	śuddhim / pañcendriyaviśuddhiṃ pratilabhate / āryāvalokiteśvaravara nityaṃ varado bhavati / nityañ ca darśanakāmatā bhavati / sarvvatathāgatadarśanā vibhaviṣyati / nityaṃ bodhisatvasamanvākṛto<r> bhaviṣyati / sarvvadevatā cāsya rakṣāvaraṇaguptaye / samvidhāśyanti / śakrabrahmādayopasaṃkramanti / catvāraś ca mahārājāna sahalaparivārā
6.	taṃ pṛthivīpradeśam upaśaṃkramanti satatasamitaṃ rakṣāvaraṇaguptaye samvidhāsyanti / nārāyaṇamaheśvaramaṇibhadra-r- anekai guhyaśatasahasrāṇi rakṣāvaraṇaguptaye samvidhāśyanti / śāntisvastyayanaṃ kariṣyanti / amoghajñānaṃ / amoghaprajñā amoghaśrutiśīlavīryatā pratilabhate / amoghadānadātā pratilabhate / amoghapuṇyaskandhasamanvāgato bhaviṣyanti yaduta ṣaṭpāramitāparipūrir bhaviṣyati /
7.	aparimitariddhikuśalaś ca bhaviṣyati / siddhividyākalpaś ca bhaviṣyati / amoghabalavīryaparākramo bhaviṣyati / amoghabuddhakṣetraguṇavyūhasampado bhaviṣyati / catuḥṣaṣṭi tathāgatagaṅgānadīvālukāsamā niyutaśatasahasrāṇi kusalamūlaparipūriṃ saṃgrahaṇā bhaviṣyati / sarvvasatvāś ca pūjāsatkaraṇīyo bhaviṣyati / sarvve vaśagatā bhaviṣyanti / vandanīyaś caityagarbhasamaś ca bhaviṣyatīti // //

4.	kāraśabdam udīrayati / sādhu sādhu vidyādhara mahāpuṇyaskandhas te prasūtaḥ / mahākuśalamūlāvaropitas tvaṃ vidyādharaḥ / cyutas tvaṃ vidyādharaḥ / dvānavatīnān tathāgatakoṭīniyutaśatasahasrasyāntike kuśalamūlaḥ sahabhāvatāyā upapāduka mama buddhakṣetreṣu upapatsyate / nivāraṇas tvaṃ vidyādharaḥ sarvvapāpaprahīṇaḥ / siddhas tvayā amoghapāśa-
5.	padmoṣṇīṣahṛdayaguhyamaṇdalaṃ mahāmudrapaṭale kuruṣva sarvvakāryāṇi yatheṣṭaṃ sa manasi varttamānaṃ tat sarvvaṃ susiddha sarvvasādhanam iti // [266] oṃ padmāvalokite // O // ṣaddhā(ḍḍhā)raṇyāṃ śatasahasrajāpena ṣaṭabhūmyā(ḍbhūmya)dhiṣṭhitam ākrāmati / sarvvatathāgatānām āloka karoti / trisāhasramahāsāhasro lokadhātuparamāṇurajaḥsamā tathāgato
6.	rhantaḥ samyaksaṃbuddha vyavalokayaṃti / sammukhadarśanaṃ<ñ> ca dāśyanti samāśvāsayanti / atha padmāvalokitadhvaja tathāgatā 'rhan samyaksaṃbuddhas tam vidyādharam āśvāsayati / ehi vidyādhara mama buddhakṣetre yādva(va)d bodhimaṇda<la>paryavasānaṃ supariniṣṭhitakarmakṛtam / pithitas tvayā narakadvārāṇi apāvṛtas te sarvvabuddhakṛtadvārāṇi / adhigatan te sarvvatathāgatabījakulasāmā-
7.	nyaṃ siddha tvayā amoghapāda(śa)padmoṣṇīṣahṛdayaguhyamaṇḍale / apāvṛtas te sarvvatra bhavanadvārāṇi laukikalokottarāṇi karmasādhanāni / yathā manasi varttamānaṃ sarvvakarmāṇi kuruṣva iti // [267] oṃ padmajvāla hūṃ dhṛk // O // sama[na]ntarasaptākṣaraśatasahasrajāpena padmavimalavigatāvaraṇaśuddhin nāma samādhiṃ pratilabhate // yena samādhinā labdena dedī-
96b(P.153a7, D.184b1)
1.	pyamānaṃ śarīraṃ nānāvarṇṇaraśmayo bhaviṣyati / saptamībhūmipratiṣthito bhaviṣyati ye ca śarīraraśmyāvabhāsena trisāhasramahāsāhasraṃ lokadhātuṃ sarvvabuddhakṣetrabhavanāni avabhāsayati / yatra yatra raśminipātena nānāratnavicitraratnojvalai vividhāni puṣpavarṣāṇi prādurbhū(bha)vanti / sarvvatathāgatapūjā pravarttante // atha vimalapadmājvalaraśmis tathāgato 'rhat samyaksaṃbuddhah suva-
2.	rṇṇavarṇṇaṃ bāhūṃ prasārya dakṣiṇapāṇinā vidyādharasya mūrdhni sthāpayati / sādhukārañ ca dāśyati / [sa]māśvāsayati / sādhu sādhu vidyādharaḥ / eṣa paramapūjājāpavidhi buddhānāṃ bhagavatā hṛdayaguhyaṃ / eṣa āryāvalokiteśvarasadbhūtahṛdayaguhyaṃ sāramaṇḍalaṃ / amoghapāda(śa)padmoṣṇīṣahṛdaya siddha tvayā vidyādhara sakalamaṇḍalasamāptaṃ padmoṣṇīṣahṛdayakalpaṃ / amoghapāśavi-
3.	kurvvitaṃ / gaccha vidyādhara mama buddhakṣetre buddhakārya kuruṣva iti // O // [268] oṃ amoghamaṇipadme // aṣṭamīhṛdayadhāraṇīśatasahasrajāpena supariśuddhakāyavimalo bhavati jāpamātrayā / yathā sphaṭikamahāmaṇi<ni>ratnaṃ supariśuddham antarbbahiḥ / evaṃ vidyādharasya śarīrapariśuddhir bhaviṣyati / aṣṭamyāṃ bhūmi pratiṣṭhito bhaviṣyati / āryāvalokiteśva-
4.	ro bodhisatvo mahāsatvaḥ supariśuddhenātmabhāvena svābhāvikarūpadarśanena vidyādharasya purata tiṣṭhati / vidyādharasya samāśvāsayati / dakṣiṇapāṇi mūrddhaśire sthāpayati / sādhukārañ ca dāśyati / sādhu sādhu vidyādhara paramādhyāśayapratipanna te vidyādhara āgaccha vidyādhara samanubaddhalokavilokitāyāṃ buddhakṣetre / tatra tvayā buddha-
5.	kṛtyaṃ bhaviṣyati<ti> / siddha tvayā amoghapāsapadmoṣṇīṣahṛdayamaṇḍalaguhyaṃ kuruṣva sarvvakarmāṇi laukikalokottaraparamasādhanam iti // O // [269] oṃ padmalocane huru hūṃ // navamyā hṛdayadhāraṇyāṃ śatasahasrajāpayā navamībhūmipratiṣṭhito bhaviṣyati / āryāvalokiteśvarasya jyeṣṭhaputro bhaviṣyati / navanavatitathāgatakoṭīniyuta-
6.	śatasahasrāṇi tathāgatasyārhataḥ samyaksaṃbuddhasya vidyādharasya sammukhadarśanaṃ nānābuddhaviśa(ṣa)yavikurvvitaṃ / darśayati / vidyā[dha]rasya sarvve pāṇi mūrddha sthāpayanti / sādhukārañ ca dāśyaṃti / sādhu sādhu mahāvidyādhara kṛtas tvayā laukikaiokottaraṃ buddhatvasādhane amoghapāśamaṇḍalakalpavidhisasūtropadeśavidhinā sarvvam etāti(ni) siddhāni ye nirdiṣṭā lokanāyakam iti
7.	// [270] oṃ padmoṣṇīṣavimale hūṃ phaṭ // O // daśākṣaraṃ hṛdayamantrasya lakṣajāpañ ca kurvvataḥ / avaivarttika<ṃ>tāṃ sthitvā daśabhūmipratiṣṭhitaḥ / tathāgataiṣa vaktavyaḥ / devamānuṣīprajāḥ / avinivarttanīyo na(bha)vaty anuttarasyāṃ samyaksaṃbodhau amoghapadmoṣṇīṣacakrapravarttanaṃ bhaviṣyati na śaṃsayam iti // daśamantraṃ sādhanā hy eṣā tatvasiddhir anuttarā jāpamātrāṇi sidhyante amogha-
97a(P.154b6, D.185b2)
1.	pāśahṛdayavikurvvitam samākhyātā mayā sarvve pūrvvam adhyavasānikaṃ / śucinā saucasamācāreṇa śucivastrāṇi dhārakaḥ / maitracittakaruṇodbhāva satyavādī guruvatsala tṛratnapraśādaparo nityaṃ guru.ācāryapūjakah / eṣa jāpavidhiḥ khyātā buddhabodhir anuttaram iti // O // atha khalu vajrapāṇir mahāyakṣasenāpati āryāvalokiteśvara bodhisatvo
2.	mahāsatvaḥ prahasitavadanā vajraṃ bhrāmayamānaṃ praṇataśirasām etad avocat / atidurlabho [']yaṃ mahāsatva idaṃ daśadhāraṇīmantrapadā amoghapāśapadmoṣṇīṣamaṇdalahṛdayamahāvipulaguhya // buddhakṛtyaiṣa ekaikaya hṛdayā dhāraṇīhṛdayaṃ jāpamātrayā kariṣyanti / kiṃ punar avalokiteśvara ye bahutarakaṃ śramaklama jāpasādhanavidhivistaraṃ sādhayanti / ahorātro-
3.	pavāsabhaikṣāhāraphalakandamūlabhojanāni bhuktvāyaṃ vidyā sādhayiṣyati / tasya kiṃ vada(ava)lokeśvara siddhir upajāyate / avalokiteśvara āha / śṛṇu vajrapāṇe ahorātropavāsajāpamātreṇa ekadivasamātreṇa phalasiddhim upajāyate // yaḥ kaścid vajrapāṇe kulaputro vā kuladuhitā vā bhikṣubhikṣuṇyupāśakopāśikā anyair vvā ka[ści]n muṇḍa-
4.	śramaṇa kula-m-upajīvībrāhmaṇakṣatriyastrīpura(ru)ṣadārakadārikāviṭchūdrā vā ekaksanalavamū(mu)hūrttam vā ekasandhyam vā trisandhyaparipūrṇṇam vā anālāpataḥ / idaṃ amoghapāśapadmoṣṇīṣahṛdayakrodhakulamantrapaṭalaṃ daśamahādhāraṇyāṃ hṛdayam ekavārā smariṣyati vā / vācaiṣyati vā nāmodgrahaṇam api śmariṣyati / tasya vajrapāṇe ekasandhyam a-
5.	pi smaraṇamātreṇa pañcānantaryakārakam api avīciparāyaṇaṃ kalpasthāyī tasya sarvva visudhyaṃte / sarvvāvaraṇasaṃcitāni pāpāvaraṇāni kṣapayiṣyanti / pariśuddhanirmalakāyaś ca bhaviṣyanti / vigatāvaraṇapāpaś ca bhaviṣyanti / cyutajanmaparivartte sukhāvatīlokadhātu-m upapādukapadmebhyo prajāsyate / jātījatismaraś ca bhaviṣyati / na ca
6.	bhūya kadāci[d] durggatigamanaṃ bhaviṣyati / aśītikalpaśatasahasrāṇi varacakravarttī bhaviṣyati / na ca bhūya kadācid durgatigamanaṃ bhaviṣyati / aśītikalpaśatasahasrāṇi sukhāvatīlokadhātu su[kha]m anubhukte dvāṣaṣṭikalpaśatasahasrāṇi abhiratyāṃ lokadhātu sukham anubhūyate / dvāsapta<tī>tikalpaśatasahasrāṇi lokavilokitāyāṃ lokadhātu-m upapadyate / jātījā-
7.	tismaraś ca bhaviṣyati / dvādaśakalpaśatasahasrāṇi maṇindharacakravarttī bhaviṣyati / aṣṭādaśakalpasahasrāṇi potalake parvvate āryāvalokiteśvarabhavane sukham anubhūyate / navānavatikalpaśatasahasrāṇi akaniṣṭheṣu deveṣūpapadyate / aśītikalpaśatasahasrāṇi tuṣiteṣu deveṣu sukham anubhūyate / kalpaśatasahasrāṇi trayastriṃṣeṣu deveṣū śakratvaṃ
97b(P.155a1, D.186b2)
1. kariṣyati / dvāpañcāśatakalpaśatasahasrāṇi daśasu dikṣu sarvvabuddhakṣetreṣu-m ākrāmati / viṅśatikalpasahasrāṇi cakravarttirājyaṃ paribhuñjate / na ca bhūyaḥ kadācin mātugarbhāvāsam upapadyate yatra yatra-m upapadyate sarvvatra-m upapādukaṃ prajāsyate / sarvvatra jātījātismaraś ca bhaviṣyati / evaṃ vajrapāṇe ekavidyāsmaraṇamātreṇa kuśalamūlamahāpuṇyaskandhasamanvāgato bhavi-
2.	ṣyati / yadi ka[ś]cid vajrapāṇe ekarātroṣitaṃ<m> vā ekopavāsajāpaṃ vā kariṣyati / tasya vajrapāṇe ihaiva janmani / iha mānuṣyaka-m-ātmabhāve nādhigatāvaraṇamahāmoghavimala nāma samādhiṃ pratilabhate / amoghavyūhasamādhiṃ pratilabhate / padmojvalavikurvvaṇasamādhiṃ pratilabhate / amoghavimalanirbhāsaṃ nāma samādhiṃ pratilabhate / amoghavilokitadhvajaṃ nāma samā-
3.	dhiṃ pratila[bha]te / padmoṣṇīṣaviratadhvajaṃ nāma samādhiṃ pratilabhate / amoghajvalanoṣṇīṣahṛdayaguhyaṃ siddhiṃ pratilabhate / dvānavatīnāṃ ca tathāgatakoṭīniyutaśatasahasrāṇi gaṅgānadīvālukāsamānāṃ tathāgatasyāntike kuśalamūlāvaropito bhaviṣyati / mahāpuṇyaskandhasamanvāgato bhaviṣyati janmaparivartte sukhāvatīlokadhā-
4.	tu-m upapādukapadmebhyo prajāsyate / ayaṃ ca amoghapāśahṛdayakalpaṃ / amoghapadmoṣṇīṣamaṇdalaṃ vimokṣamaṇḍalakalpaṃ sakalasamāptaṃ mudrākalpa sakalasamāptaṃ mukhāgre [']vatiṣṭhanti / amoghavidyādharacakravarttī bhaviṣyatīti // O // amoghapāśahṛdayapadmoṣṇīṣamaṇdale hṛdayamantrasādhanavidhiḥ / dvitīyasādhanavidhiṃ //
